first diagnostic found the pop-in is a mount-fetch loading-state defect app-wide, worst on Game Plan (36.6% late-DOM). #826 POC'd a **data-gated whole-screen entrance** for agent Game Plan (hold the fade until `!loading && !error`, 400ms cap) — datacenter-only measurement initially looked clean but field re-measurement showed the cap still fired on 100% of cold loads at Slow 4G (game-plan's critical path is ~1 Firestore round-trip). #829 (stacked on #826) added **dashboard-idle prefetch** — 3 warm `onSnapshot` listeners on the year docs, attached while the dashboard is idle so the later `getDoc` resolves from cache — and this is what actually made it field-robust: at Slow 4G, `contentReady` went from 670ms/5-of-5 cap-fires to **~23ms/0-of-5**, **0/30 visible frame-pops across the full network-latency matrix**. #831 fixed a Game Plan section-spacing regression surfaced along the way. #833 swept all 5 reachable roles (`--sweep` mode) and confirmed the pattern is pervasive (~half of data-backed tabs pop app-wide, worst on shared panels reused across roles) and that the #829 fix is **agent-only so far** — `mp-game-plan` for BM/UM still fails; full skeleton-vs-prefetch split and rollout order in `docs/audits/popin-allroles-sweep-2026-07-07.md`. #832 harvested the abandoned skeleton branch into a standalone, reduced-motion-safe **`PanelSkeleton` kit** (`src/components/ui/PanelSkeleton.jsx` — list/card-grid/metric-row/table variants) — **kit only, not yet wired into any live panel**. #830 recon'd React Query adoption for the rollout and recommended **DON'T-ADOPT now** — the pop-in is a loading-state defect that skeletons fix directly; React Query would be an optional caching layer, revisit only on a real caching trigger (see `docs/FOLLOW_UPS.md`). Net shipped to live panels from this program: the agent Game Plan gating+prefetch (#826/#829) and the spacing fix (#831) — the skeleton rollout itself remains unbuilt. *(Archived when the Runs 5-7 promotion/#858 entry became the 6th "Recently shipped" row, 2026-07-15.)* |

## Last updated — archived entries (PR #687 and older; plus #783 archived 2026-07-03; plus #785 archived 2026-07-04; plus #786 archived 2026-07-04; plus #794–#799 archived 2026-07-05; plus #849 archived 2026-07-10; plus #974 archived 2026-09-26; plus #976 archived 2026-09-26)

**L0 two-layer rings SHIPPED (2026-09-26), PR #980 merge `0fcf215d` (feature-branch HEAD `28787ef2`).** First item of the Policy Ledger autorun (`docs/briefs/autorun-2026-09-26-ledger.md`, build brief `docs/briefs/ledger-lens-build.md`), merged by the orchestrator under Kyron's one-time L0-L3 authorisation. `ProgressDonut` gains an optional `pending` value: faint arc = settled + pending behind, solid arc = settled in front, both clamped at 100%; `RingLegend` "Settled — counts" / "Submitted — waiting to settle" and a "+x submitted" sub-line, hidden when pending is 0. Wired on the Home hero (new `deriveYearProduction().pending` = gone in, not settled, not ntu/denied/lapsed, dated like `submitted`, family included), the Home campaign card and the Campaign screen API + Applications rows (new `pendingValue`/`pendingApps` on lens `state:'pending'` contributions + `derivePolicyLens().pending`; existing fields unchanged). Persistency ring unchanged. **Gates:** lint 0/0; tests 413 files / 6,978 passing; build OK; CI (`lint-and-build`, `functions-tests`, `firestore-rules-tests`, `a11y-contrast`) all pass on `28787ef2`; CodeRabbit summary only, 0 findings, then rate-limited. Design check: preview (A11Y agent, empty state) + local fixture renders, screenshots `docs/reports/screenshots/ledger-2026-09-26/l0/`. Production smoke (390 + 1440 light, read-only): PASS, no console errors. **No deploy-gated surface touched.** **Prior:**

**Profile photo Storage rules merged AND DEPLOYED (2026-09-25), PR #976 merge `0c8b7373` (feature-branch HEAD `eae48865`).** `storage.rules` opens one path, `avatars/{tenantId}/{fileName}`: same-tenant read; owner-only create/update of `{uid}.jpg`, `image/jpeg`, under 2 MB; no delete; the catch-all deny stays for every other path. Fixes profile-photo upload, broken in production since SEC-12 copied the live deny-all rules. `tests/rules/storage.rules.test.mjs` rewritten to a 12-case matrix, 12/12 in the emulator. `ProfileScreen.jsx` unchanged - its upload `catch` already shows an error and clears the spinner. **DEPLOYED by CC on the operator's instruction in chat - QUERIED, not inferred:** `firebase deploy --only storage` logged `released rules storage.rules to firebase.storage` after the 21:45:08Z merge; a live REST probe then read the test agent's own avatar path as 404 (allowed, no file yet) and `uploads/probe.jpg` as 403, stable 5 of 5 after a short propagation flap. **Production smoke `scripts/verification/smoke-profile-photo-976.mjs`: 7 PASS / 0 FAIL** - the A11Y agent uploads a generated JPEG (light), the photo survives a refresh, a fresh dark session shows it, and the same-tenant branch manager reads it (200) and sees it rendered on the Persistency roster. **Known limit:** `getDownloadURL` token URLs bypass the rules by Firebase design (accepted for avatars). Also banked a LOW FU: Tenant Admin has no Persistency view. *(Archived to history when PR #978 became the 4th "Last updated" entry, 2026-09-26.)*

**Security S2 merged (2026-09-25), PR #974 merge `fe532693` (feature-branch final HEAD `3c366aff`).** Closes SEC-10, SEC-13, SEC-15 and PERF-01 from the 2026-09-24 audit — the last of the 3-PR security-phase-0-1 brief; all 3 PRs (S0 #972, S1 #973, S2 #974) now merged. SEC-10: sign-out races `waitForPendingWrites(db)` (5s timeout) before `terminate(db)`→`clearIndexedDbPersistence(db)`→reload; if writes are still pending it shows a confirm ("You have changes that haven't synced yet. Sign out anyway?") — Cancel signs out nobody and clears nothing. New `firestoreRecovery.js` + `FirestoreCorruptionBoundary` catch the `INTERNAL ASSERTION FAILED` family (the live 2026-09-23 `ID: b815` stuck-load bug) at app start and via an error boundary, offering a "Repair app data" button that clears ONLY the Firestore IndexedDB (never the auth store) and reloads; the loading screen self-offers Repair after 20s too. SEC-13: `vercel.json` adds HSTS, `X-Content-Type-Options: nosniff`, `X-Frame-Options: DENY` (confirmed safe — a repo-wide iframe grep is empty; the kiosk only ever opens as its own `--kiosk` browser window, never embedded), `Referrer-Policy`, and CSP as `Content-Security-Policy-Report-Only` built from what the app actually loads (Firebase, Fontshare, Clarity, Vercel) — enforcing it is Kyron's call after a week of reports. SEC-15: `neutralizeCsvFormula` now prefixes only text cells starting with `=`/`+`/`-`/`@`/tab/CR that are NOT purely numeric, so a real negative or thousands-separated number (`-5000`, `-5,000.00`, `1,234.56`) stays a number in the export. PERF-01: `FinancingSelfView` is `React.lazy()`-loaded — it was a static import shared by both `AgentDashboard` and `ManagerDashboard` (themselves already lazy from `App.jsx`), so it was fetched on every dashboard load whether or not the tab opened; net ~626 kB / ~148 kB gz removed from the eager path, replaced by a ~19 kB / ~5.7 kB gz chunk fetched on open. **Dispatcher-authorized in-PR extension (Rule 9), same PR, second round:** the pending-writes sign-out confirm and CSV numeric-preservation rule above were both added after the first merge review, plus a from-source re-verification that `X-Frame-Options: DENY` cannot break the kiosk (commits `20f09ef4`/`3c366aff`). **Tests: 6,874 total across 408 files, 0 failing** (full-parallel-run timeout flakes on unrelated files, isolated-rerun-verified). **No deploy-gated surface touched** — `git diff --stat 0ad29f36..fe532693 -- firestore.rules firestore.indexes.json functions/ storage.rules` is EMPTY; ships entirely via the Vercel rebuild at merge, no manual Firebase deploy. **Found and banked, not fixed:** the "Repair app data" escape hatch has no pending-writes safeguard of its own (CodeRabbit, MEDIUM) — Repair exists because the client may already be broken, so blindly copying signOut's gate could itself hang; a dispatcher UX call, not this PR's to make. *(Archived to history when PR #979 became the 4th "Last updated" entry, 2026-09-26.)*


**Security Phase 0 (S0) merged (2026-09-25), PR #972 `82a38088` (feature-branch HEAD `73304a54`).** Adds a `firestore-rules-tests` CI job (`firebase emulators:exec --only firestore` + Node's built-in test runner) that runs all 38 rules test files — `tests/rules/**` plus root `firestore.rules.test.mjs` — on every PR; the existing `vite.config.js` exclusion of those files from default `npm test` is unchanged. **On `main` before this PR: 32/38 pass, 6 fail — all 6 were rotted test harness/data bugs, zero exposed a real rules hole:** hardcoded emulator port 8080 vs this repo's 9090 (2 files), a missing `node:test` import, a same-value write that vacuously passes an empty `affectedKeys()` check (`user-license`), a stale field name `contactsMade` vs `telContacts` (`pm1-deny-matrix`), and two cases (`moneyNeeds`, `weeklyPlans`) where a unit_manager wrote to their OWN doc — allowed by design via `isProducingManager()` — while asserting DENY. No `firestore.rules` edit, no `.skip` needed. CI job runtime: 2m24s. No `firestore.rules`, no `functions/` changed (only the rules TEST file). Smoke waived — CI/test-tooling only, no user-visible runtime path. **Review found one test-coverage gap, not a rules bug:** `moneyNeeds` has no ALLOW case for a unit/branch manager creating their OWN worksheet (`weeklyPlans` already has the equivalent) — banked as a FOLLOW_UP for security S1. **First of a 3-PR security brief** (`docs/briefs/security-phase-0-1.md`, sourced from `docs/audits/agencytrack-audit-2026-09-24.md`): **S1 (rules/functions hardening — SEC-01/03/07/12/14) is NEXT**, blocked on Kyron pasting the live Storage rules to `docs/briefs/storage-rules-live.txt`; S2 (client hardening) follows S1.

**Hero-ledger H3 merged (2026-09-23), PR #970 `d59ab6a9`. Campaign hero card ships on the Awards tab and on HomeV2, closing the hero-ledger-truth brief (H1 #968, H2 #969, H3 #970 — all three merged).** New `src/components/campaigns/CampaignHeroCard.jsx` reads `derivePolicyLens` — the SAME call `CampaignLensPanel` makes (C-D10), never weekly submissions — showing API / Applications / 24-Month Persistency against the tier in reach (`lens.tierNext`/`.tierReached`, C2 item 6). Placed above `HeroAwardCard` on the Awards tab (fed from `AgentAwardsPanel`'s own already-fetched `ledgerPolicies`, no new read) and directly under `HeroCard` on Home, REPLACING the old per-campaign `CampaignCard` there — which closes the "TTD 0 / 275,000" weekly-submission-bar gap H1/H2 both banked (`CampaignCard`'s `MetricBar` totalled `computeCampaignProgress(campaign, submissions, ...)`, never the ledger). `CampaignCard.jsx` itself is left in place, now unused, removal out of this brief's stated scope. **Same PR folds in the H2-banked FOLLOW_UP:** `AwardCard`'s state pill fell through to NOT STARTED for any award neither qualified nor in contention, even with real progress (Kyron's live MDRT read "NOT STARTED · 17%") — a fourth IN PROGRESS state now covers 0 < progress < contention, exactly 0 still reads NOT STARTED; `ManagerAwardsPanel.jsx` reuses the same shared `AwardCard` and needed the identical test fix. **Pre-merge review (Kyron, same day) found the persistency row could show a fabricated 0%** — a binary gate's 0% reads as disqualified, a claim the card had no evidence for on a tenant with no persistency record yet. Fixed in a second commit before merge: the row shows "—" with no bar fill when the figure is unknown, and — when the actual gate reading isn't available — previews the latest 24-month-model month on file ("54% as at August 2026"), labelled "Gate judged on \<month\> · \<threshold\>% needed", styled with Nexus warning tokens when the preview sits below the gate. **Investigated, not assumed:** the Home pulse-strip's own "Persistency 54%" (`HomeV2/index.jsx` ~line 146) aggregates every record in the CURRENT CALENDAR YEAR, blending legacy-12-month records (before Sep 2026, `PERSISTENCY_MODEL_24M_EFFECTIVE_FROM`) with 24-month ones into one year-to-date figure — NOT the 24-month model the brief asks for — so the preview does not reuse that source; it filters to `isTwentyFourMonthModel(monthKey)` records first, then reduces the single latest one through the same `aggregatePersistency()` math everyone else uses. The actual gate figure always wins over the preview, proven by a test with both a December `finalMonth` reading and an earlier preview-eligible record on file. **Two guard-test updates in `excludeImported.test.js`** (the H1-item-6 prop-provenance / raw-carrier guards): the HomeV2 manifest entry renamed `<CampaignCard policies>` → `<CampaignHeroCard policies>`; a new classified entry added for `AgentAwardsPanel.jsx :: <CampaignHeroCard policies>` (provenance: unfiltered, fed from `ledgerPolicies` — the same raw ledger read already used for `awardRowsFromLedger` under R5/H2). **Smoke walk on Kyron's live account, light + dark, two runs, 20 PASS / 0 FAIL each:** the first confirmed API TTD 73,946.28 / TTD 275,000, Apps 3 / 35, and the old weekly-submission bars gone; the second (card only, post persistency-fix) confirmed the row reads "— / 90%" live — Kyron's tenant holds no 24-month-model persistency record yet, so the dash renders instead of a fabricated zero, and the preview/warning paths are unit-tested rather than live-exercised. All screenshots checked for client names/policy numbers before each commit — none present. **CodeRabbit posted a summary only both times (Free plan, rate-limited to 1 review/hour) — no line comments to disposition.** **MERGED; NO DEPLOY-GATED SURFACE — QUERIED, `git diff --stat 0877d129..d59ab6a9 -- firestore.rules firestore.indexes.json functions/` returns EMPTY.** **The hero-ledger-truth brief is now fully shipped end to end (H1/H2/H3).**

**Nexus v2 promoted staging → production (2026-07-09/10, PR #849 merge `2c2932f3`).** Runs 1+2 of the Nexus v2 redesign (96 commits) went live in prod. Backend deployed first from the staging worktree: Firestore indexes (declined deletion of `prospectInfo` DESC + `jointCalls` composites), rules (+221 lines — `cro` role + Arm E, `appointments`, `recruitingCandidates`, WAR reviewer arm, `weeklyPlans.contactsMade→telContacts`), all 26 Cloud Functions redeployed (only logic delta: CREATION_MATRIX + `cro`), and the prod kiosk IAM grant (appspot SA → `serviceAccountTokenCreator`, unconditional). Verification-hygiene (VH) suite ran clean at the promoted staging tip (`24fa445f`): 32/32 clean + `t2-financing-k9-k7` cleared 3/3 isolated re-runs after an initial first-paint flake (33/33 effective). Phase 3 prod verification passed: feature flags fail-closed (`persistencyV2`/`policyLedgerCampaignLens`/`awardsProvenance` shells absent, no stray `featureFlags` field), agent + admin click-throughs clean, drag-reorder persisted, consoles clean incl. Financing/Report, motion accepted by eyeball (the `motion-verifier` script itself was skipped — `A11Y_*` creds unset in the promotion session). ## Where we left off — archived entries

**PRIOR - 2026-09-26 (afternoon). L0 two-layer rings merged as `0fcf215d` (#980) - first item of the Policy Ledger autorun.**

The Policy Ledger autorun (`docs/briefs/autorun-2026-09-26-ledger.md`) is running L0 → L3 serially, each merged by the orchestrator under Kyron's one-time authorisation, frontend-only.

**L0 shipped.** The live donuts now show two layers: settled (solid) and submitted-but-not-settled (faint, behind). "Pending" is its own engine figure, not submitted minus settled: that subtraction mixes submit-date and issue-date bases. Campaign pending is exactly the lens's existing `state:'pending'` set, now carrying its would-be credit.

**Not seen live:** the test agent has no policies and no campaign, so every ring with pending was checked on local fixture renders only (A11Y-campaign FU still open). Banked LOW FU: hero legend value form vs C1. **Next:** L1 award lens.

**PRIOR - 2026-09-25. Security S1 merged as `683c32af` (#973) AND DEPLOYED the same morning - SEC-01, SEC-03, SEC-07, SEC-12 and SEC-14 are closed in production. QUERIED: rules 12:49:57Z, storage 12:50:26Z, functions 12:56:08Z, `setUserClaims` deleted 12:53:08Z.**

S1 is the second of three PRs in `docs/briefs/security-phase-0-1.md`. It closes the audit's one Critical (`setUserClaims`, which took role and tenant claims straight from the request), stops unit managers minting never-expiring kiosk sessions for any branch (client writes to `kioskTokens` denied; the CF branch-checks and always sets a 90-day `expiresAt`), and stops any agent or kiosk listing every agent's persistency. Storage rules are now in the repo, copied verbatim; email templates escape HTML.

**The one design choice worth knowing (SEC-07).** The list rule mirrors `allow get`: one `get()` on the agent's user doc per id. Production caps a query at 10 rule `get()` calls, so manager queries carry at most 9 agent ids (`PERSISTENCY_QUERY_BATCH`). The emulator is more lenient (it allowed 15), so the cap came from the Firebase docs - and the post-deploy manager check then exercised it for real: the `tatillife_smoke` branch has 14 users, so its month list ran as 9 + 5 batches, with 0 permission errors. The alternative (copy `branchId` onto every persistency doc) was not taken because it needs a backfill.

**Deployed by CC under a one-time operator authorisation for PR #973 only.** The functions deploy was probed non-interactively first; it listed `setUserClaims` as the ONLY deletion (the `firestore-send-email` extension function was not on the list), so exactly that function was deleted with `functions:delete`, then the deploy ran with nothing to delete. Post-deploy: agent smoke 24 PASS / 0 FAIL on Kyron's account; branch-manager check shows June 2026 figures for 4 of 6 agents (the only month with data), branch 80.8%, no console errors.

**What is left.** Re-mint any kiosk link whose token doc has no `expiresAt` (links minted by the old CF carry a 1-year expiry and keep working until then). **S2 (client hardening) is next.** Banked for a ruling: the live Storage rules deny everything, so profile-photo upload is likely broken in production. *(Archived when PR #979's CURRENT entry pushed this PRIOR block over the 3-entry cap, 2026-09-26.)*

**PRIOR - 2026-09-23 (later still). Hero-ledger H3 merged as `d59ab6a9` (#970) - the hero-ledger-truth brief (H1/H2/H3) is now fully shipped. MERGED; NO DEPLOY-GATED SURFACE - QUERIED across `0877d129..d59ab6a9`.**

The goal in one line: give Kyron one hero card, reused verbatim on the Awards tab and on Home, that shows his campaign standing from the SAME ledger derivation the Policy Ledger's own lens panel reads - closing the gap H1 and H2 both banked, that the campaign card's own progress bars still totalled weekly submissions and read "TTD 0 / 275,000" against a real TTD 73,946.28. `CampaignHeroCard.jsx` calls `derivePolicyLens` directly - the same call `CampaignLensPanel` makes, not a copy - so the hero can never disagree with the ledger panel about the level in reach. It replaces `CampaignCard` on Home entirely; `CampaignCard.jsx` itself is left in place, unused, since deleting it and its tests was outside this brief's five-item checklist.

**The same PR closed the H2-banked FOLLOW_UP as instructed by the dispatcher's own prior ruling.** `AwardCard`'s state pill fell through to NOT STARTED for any award neither qualified nor "in contention" (a threshold well above zero), so a real 17% or 34% of progress read as if nothing existed. A fourth IN PROGRESS state now covers `0 < progressPercent` below contention; exactly 0 still reads NOT STARTED, per the FOLLOW_UP's own stated fix shape. `HeroAwardCard` and `AwardDrillDrawer` were checked for the same fall-through and carry no literal "NOT STARTED" string, so nothing else needed the fix. `ManagerAwardsPanel.jsx` reuses the identical shared `AwardCard`, so its own test needed the same correction.

**Kyron reviewed the open PR before merging and found a real defect the smoke had not caught: the persistency row could show a bare 0%.** On a binary gate, 0% reads as a disqualifying result - a claim the card had no evidence for on a tenant with no persistency record covering the campaign window yet. Fixed in a second commit, before merge: the row now shows "—" with no bar fill whenever the figure is genuinely unknown, and - when the true gate reading (`persistencyPctForGate`, unchanged) is not yet available - previews the latest month actually on the 24-month model, labelled with its own month and a "Gate judged on \<month\> · \<threshold\>% needed" note, styled in Nexus warning tokens when that preview sits below the gate. **Checked, not assumed, per the dispatcher's own instruction:** the Home pulse-strip's "Persistency 54%" aggregates every record in the current calendar year regardless of model, blending legacy-12-month figures (anything before September 2026, per `PERSISTENCY_MODEL_24M_EFFECTIVE_FROM`) into one blended number - which is not "the 24-month persistency" the instruction asked for, so the preview does not read from that source; it filters to 24-month-model records first, then reduces the single latest one through the exact same `aggregatePersistency()` math every other reader uses. A test with both a December `finalMonth` reading and an earlier preview-eligible record on file proves the real gate figure always wins.

**Smoke walk on Kyron's live account, light + dark, run twice** (`scripts/verification/smoke-campaign-hero-h3.mjs`): 20 PASS / 0 FAIL both times. The first run (before the persistency fix) confirmed API TTD 73,946.28 / TTD 275,000, Apps 3 / 35, and that the old weekly-submission bars are gone from Home. The second run (card only, after the fix) confirmed the persistency row reads "— / 90%" live rather than a fabricated "0% / 90%" - Kyron's tenant has no persistency record on the 24-month model yet, so the preview/warning paths remain unit-tested rather than live-exercised. Every screenshot was checked for client names or policy numbers before each commit; none were present.

**Bot review was thin, as it has been on every hero-ledger PR.** CodeRabbit posted a summary comment only, both times - Free-plan rate limit, one included review per hour, nothing left to disposition under Rule 21.

**What is left.** The hero-ledger-truth brief (`docs/briefs/hero-ledger-truth.md`) is now fully shipped - H1, H2 and H3 all merged. The only inherited gap is the hero-refresh-without-reload extension from H1, still wired for the agent dashboard only. The next authorised-but-unstarted item on the OTHER track is **C4** (manager Table 2, the Rule 9 clawback flag, Rule 10 award-cash suppression, the page-8 general rules on screen), unchanged by this cycle - see below.


**PRIOR - 2026-09-23 (later). Hero-ledger H2 merged as `1e11653c` (#969). Awards count current-year imported business by issue date, amending ruling 5e - self/family excluded except MDRT. MERGED; NO DEPLOY-GATED SURFACE - QUERIED across `594be38a..1e11653c`.**

The goal in one line: an award and the H1 hero should never disagree about the same month's business. `AgentAwardsPanel.jsx` filtered its ledger read with `excludeImported`, the exact mechanism H1's brief and C-D10 already found wrong for the hero and the campaign lens - on Kyron's 100%-imported book it hid every settled 2026 policy and every award read zero.

**`awardRowsFromLedger()` is the new R5 date test for awards, in `ledgerProduction.js` beside H1's `deriveYearProduction`.** Each settled Life policy is bucketed by the month of its `dateIssued`, never by `importSource`; API and apps come from the SAME R3 credit helper the H1 hero uses (`productionCredit`), so a policy contributes the identical figure to both surfaces. **A second, less obvious defect surfaced in Phase 1:** the old award-row shape read `settledAPI`, and every imported doc carries `settledAPI: null` - dropping the origin filter alone, without also fixing the money source, would have shown TTD 0 for three real August policies. Source selection also changed: the ledger is read when `usesPolicyLedger` is set OR the agent holds ANY ledger policy, so an unflagged agent whose book is 100% imported still sees real numbers; the flag stays for an agent whose ledger happens to be empty. The source chip and drawer provenance were updated to name the source ACTUALLY read, not the flag - they had drifted apart the moment source selection stopped being flag-only.

**The dispatcher's own review of the first smoke changed the ruling mid-PR.** The initial build counted a self/family policy (the 2026-08-07 TTD 1,200 policy) toward awards, matching both the old awards path and the H1 hero. On seeing Q3 read TTD 75,146.28 / 4 apps on screen, the operator ruled: self/family does NOT count toward any award, but DOES still count on the home hero and on MDRT. This is not a small toggle - MDRT is computed by the SAME `computeAgentAwards()` call as every other award, from the SAME confirmed-data rows, so excluding self/family from the row entirely would have silently zeroed it out of MDRT too. The fix adds a `selfFamilyAPI` field to each ledger row (self/family API goes there instead of `settledAPI`/`settledApps`) and `awardsEngine.js` adds that field back into `mdrtAPI` for MDRT alone - the one award with a documented reason to keep counting it. Every other annual/quarterly/monthly award reads `settledAPI`/`settledApps` untouched, so they exclude it automatically. Kyron's Q3 dropped from TTD 75,146.28 / 4 apps to the corrected **TTD 73,946.28 / 3 apps** on the second smoke; MDRT and the home hero stayed at TTD 87,146.28 across both smokes, proving the split works.

**One check the smoke cannot perform on any date, and the dispatcher waived it rather than leave it silently missing.** Advisor of the Month always reads the CURRENT month (`computeAgentAwards` uses `now`), so a smoke run in September shows September - TTD 0, since Kyron wrote no September business - never August. There is no month-picker anywhere in Awards. The dispatcher's Option 1 (accept the unit-test proof; the Q3 card already shows the August policies on screen) closes this without new UI work; Option 2 (a "last month" view) was named as a possible follow-up, not authorised here.

**Smoke walk on Kyron's live account, light + dark, both runs.** First run (pre-ruling): 8 PASS / 0 FAIL / 4 BLOCKED, Q3 TTD 75,146.28 / 4 apps. Second run (post-ruling): 10 PASS / 0 FAIL / 4 BLOCKED, Q3 TTD 73,946.28 / 3 apps, MDRT and the home hero both confirmed at TTD 87,146.28. The 4 blocked checks are 2 source-chip reads (the `awardsProvenance` flag is off for this tenant, so the chip never renders - unit-tested instead) and 2 August reads (waived, as above) - none are failures.

**CI hit one known flake, re-ran clean; CodeRabbit had nothing to disposition.** `lint-and-build`'s first run failed on the registered `DailyCaptureV2` streak-celebration timeout (`docs/FOLLOW_UPS.md` § #899 flake fix), unrelated to this PR's files; a re-run with zero code change went green. CodeRabbit posted a summary only, no line comments, on both commits - Free plan, no actionable items to disposition under Rule 21.

**Banked, not fixed here.** Award cards show "NOT STARTED" above 0% progress - `AwardCard`'s state pill falls through to NOT STARTED for any award that is neither qualified nor "in contention" (a threshold well above zero), so MDRT read "NOT STARTED - 17%" and Quarterly Apps "NOT STARTED - 7%" on the live screen. Scheduled for H3 by the same dispatcher ruling that closed the self/family question - `docs/FOLLOW_UPS.md` § Award card NOT STARTED above 0%.

**What is left.** **H3** (the campaign hero card on Awards and Dashboard, fed by `derivePolicyLens`, Sonnet 5) is authorised and NOT started - it dispatches next, per `docs/briefs/hero-ledger-truth.md`. It inherits the H1 gap (hero-refresh-without-reload wired for the agent dashboard only) and now also the NOT STARTED label fix.

A flag-gated surface still showing the rejected arithmetic was a false fact waiting for someone to flip `persistencyV2` on; P-D6 said retire it, and this PR did, including the flag's own entries in `featureFlagsService.js` and `companyConfigRegistry.js`.

"12-Month Persistency" does not appear anywhere in `CLAUDE.md` - the item's real target was a stale `0-100` annotation on the persistency field (FU K4), corrected to the actual `0-1` decimal fraction the code has always stored.

The stale entry is unreachable - nothing in the UI offers that path anymore - but it is real debt, not resolved debt.

### Prior session — Round displayed award progress percentage — PR #440 MERGED `5d0cb71` Demo-surfaced cleanup / bug-fix; **frontend-only, display-formatting**. The agent Awards tab rendered the awards engine's `progressPercent` as raw floats (`13.333333333333334%`, `9.538461538461538%`); the manager Awards tab shared the same latent bug (only `0%` read clean because 0 is round). Root cause (Phase 0): `awardsEngine.js:33` computes `progressPercent` as a clamped raw float (`Math.min(100, Math.max(0, (value/threshold)*100))`) and the render sites displayed it verbatim. **Fix:** a single shared `formatAwardPct(pct) = Math.round(Number(pct)||0)` in `src/utils/formatters.js`, applied at every raw-float display site: `awardPrimitives.jsx` (`AwardDonut` `{percent}%` label · `AwardCard` state pill `${progressPercent}%` · `AwardCard` big percent) and the **inline-duplicated copies** in `AgentAwardsPanel.jsx` (same three). Both the shared module and the inline dupes were fixed because of the known `awardPrimitives` ↔ `AgentAwardsPanel` duplication (a one-site fix leaves the agent panel broken) — that dedup FU stays **open and untouched**, with a cross-reference note added. **Phase-0 enumeration** also confirmed the sites that were already rounded and needed no change: `MonthlyBonusHero.fillPercent`, `AwardDrillDrawer` criteria `pct`, kiosk `AwardsWatchPanel` (own `Math.round` + `PctDisplay`), PDF `AwardRow`, `AwardMedalCard.primaryCriterionLine` (renders criterion current/target, `.toFixed(0)` for `%`; also now an orphan), and HomeV2 `MiniDonut` (SVG arc only, no numeric label). Manager award cards render through the shared `AwardCard`/`AwardDrillDrawer`, so the `awardPrimitives` edit fixes them too.

**Scope guard.** Display-only: bar-fill widths, the awards engine, and all threshold/criterion logic are unchanged. No schema / rules / functions / indexes / deploy.

**Verification.** Lint 0 errors (1 pre-existing `wizard-v2-pr1-admin-helpers.cjs` warning, untouched); **2113/2113 vitest** (+3 new `awardPrimitives.test.jsx` cases asserting the actual components whole-number a fractional percent — `AwardDonut` 13.333→`13%`, `AwardCard` 9.538→`10%` pill+big, 49.5→`50%` half-up — and emit no raw-float `/\d+\.\d{3,}\s*%/`; a formatter-only test was insufficient given the duplication); build clean. **Smoke PASS 4/4 both themes** (`scripts/verification/award-percent-rounding-smoke.mjs`): agent + manager Awards tabs both render every award percent as a whole number (`rawFloats=0`); the identical smoke run against **production main** shows the live bug (`rawFloats=12` on the agent surface), proving the fix matters; the only serious/critical axe rule is the **pre-existing** awards-card `color-contrast` debt (identical on prod main → **NO-NEW**; it's `text-ink-faint` micro-labels + the `text-[9px]` accent-on-tint state pills, tracked by the open faint→muted migration + `awardPrimitives` dedup FUs, out of scope for a display-formatting fix); 0 console errors. Brief-landing PR [#439](https://github.com/Kelsean868/agencytrack/pull/439) (`56dca33`) preceded this dispatch per Rule 10.

**Next:** unchanged — the **Game Plan v2 weekly/daily activity planner** (Path B) remains the flagged next slice; plus the standing backlog (Decision-A SUGGESTED-atom + goal-seeding, `BranchManagerProductionView`/Leaderboard podium aggregate-wiring, SEC-9b services-tenantId refactor, #412 awards FUs incl. the still-open primitives dedup, #413 kiosk rank-color, MEDIUM social-channel aggregation, the 4 Daily-Capture Slice-1 FUs, end-to-end pilot-prep walkthrough).

(2) When on-scale, the MDRT label is positioned at its true fraction `left: ${mdrtPct}%` and **vertically staggered** into its own band above the axis-endpoint row, so it can never collide with the Goal label (§1.2). (3) The goal amount is **de-duped** — the end label is a bare "Goal" tick; the amount already lives in the subtitle ("X% of TTD 200,000 goal") (§1.3 Phase-1 call). Start label "TTD 0" unchanged. **Mockup reconciliation:** the v2 mockup (`design_handoff_v2_app/mockups/app-dashboard-v2.jsx`) hero uses goal `600K` > MDRT `500K` (MDRT on-scale) and a simple 3-span legend — it never anticipated the off-scale case, so this fix aligns with (does not contradict) the mockup. No existing HeroCard tests to preserve. | [#406](https://github.com/Kelsean868/agencytrack/pull/406) | `20a2be2` | Track J movement chip — viewer week-over-week rank movement (human-merge, frontend-only). Direction (the easy-to-invert bit): `delta = previousRank − rank` — a rank IMPROVES by getting numerically smaller, so previousRank 5 → rank 2 yields ▲3 (climbed); previousRank 2 → rank 5 yields ▼3 (dropped); previousRank == rank → "–" (even, no magnitude); previousRank null → no chip (null path). **Viewer-only** by convention: each surface gates the chip render on `isViewer`. **WEEK-only** by data: the P5-prep CF writes `previousRank=null` on MTD/QTD/YTD entries → the chip self-disables on those periods via the same null code path. Nexus tokens only (`text-success bg-success-tint` / `text-danger bg-danger-tint` / `text-ink-muted bg-surface-muted`); no raw hex. 38 new tests: 15 unit tests on the direction math (▲ / ▼ / – / null + singular vs plural aria-label + large magnitudes + token mapping) + 23 surface-integration tests asserting the chip renders on the viewer's row in ALL four surfaces for ▲/▼/–/null AND does NOT render on neighbor rows (the unit tests carry ▲/▼ verification — the live test tenant has no week-over-week movement). No backend, no aggregate change, no deploy (Vercel auto-deploys frontend on merge). |

## Where we left off — archived paragraphs

**Clarity integration — SHIPPED (PR #786, `9d9aaa4d`, 2026-07-03).** Privacy-gated Microsoft Clarity (session recording + heatmaps) added to the portal — a dispatcher-initiated ad-hoc FU, off the K-track/GPM sequence, closing the "ambient uncommitted `@microsoft/clarity` dep" note banked in PR #785's fill. Landed via `/land-and-dispatch` (brief committed direct-to-main `30e884e2`) then dispatched via `/dispatch`. **Phase 0 caught a premise shift, clean halt, 0 strikes:** the brief called for "strict masking configured at init," but the `@microsoft/clarity` v1.0.2 API's `init(projectId)` takes only a project id — masking MODE is Clarity-dashboard-only, not settable in code. Dispatcher ruling: STRICT mode becomes a mandatory **operator** post-merge step; the code-enforced defense is element-level `data-clarity-mask="True"` on the three financial surfaces (MoneyNeedsPanel worksheet, TeamPlansRoster roster, AgentPlanDrawer panel), backstopped by a new static guard + a prod-gate no-op unit test (both Rule-23 red-demonstrated then restored). Prod-only init (`import.meta.env.PROD` AND `VITE_CLARITY_PROJECT_ID`) means preview smokes see zero `clarity.ms` traffic by construction — proven both themes (`window.clarity` undefined, zero network hits, mask attribute live on DOM) alongside a GPM1 Team Plans regression run (18/18, masks unaffected). Rule 21: Gemini 4/4 IMPLEMENT (1 HIGH test env-leak); CodeRabbit 1 DISAGREE (dynamic-import nitpick, rationale in PR) + 1 IMPLEMENT (in-tag attribute match). Operator's three-step post-merge sequence (create Clarity project, set dashboard masking to STRICT, set `VITE_CLARITY_PROJECT_ID` in Vercel Production + redeploy + live playback check) completed and verified — see the resolved "Clarity operator activation" item in Pending operational state. *(Archived when PR #790 `16a66bcb` became the 4th "Where we left off" paragraph, 2026-07-04.)*

**PR-GPM1 Team Plans Reader — SHIPPED (PR #785, `95529a75`, 2026-07-03)** *(archived 2026-07-04 when the Window-3-serial-run paragraph became the 4th entry).* First slice of the Game Plan manager surface (Fork A): UM/BM now get an honest read of consent-shared Money Needs worksheets — shared worksheets land in front of the manager with the derived coaching figures only (locked SHOWN/PRIVATE projection contract; every household-budget line item provably absent from the DOM), and flipping the toggle off removes them live in both directions. Executed by a dispatch-executor subagent pinned to Opus per the brief's `run_model`, dispatched via the `/land-and-dispatch` fast path (brief landed direct-to-main `3d88932c`). The brief's Phase 0.1 falsification gate fired exactly as designed: `g5-privacy-smoke` was red on harness drift (settle race + stale `tatillife_south` fixture), the executor halted with zero footprint, and the dispatcher's Option 1 ruling authorized the in-PR repair — post-repair the gate ran 5/5 fresh against production, giving the G5 UM/BM read arms their first-ever UI exercise. The merge was also the **first outing of the new Rule 21 pre-merge final poll** (banked to CLAUDE.md this session, `cc5c4b65`, from the #783/#784 near-miss) — it came back clean.

**Orchestrated window (2026-07-02) — COMPLETE, all 3 lanes shipped** *(archived 2026-07-03 when the Fork B1 paragraph became the 4th entry).* Repo-wide `dark:text-primary-dark`-as-text sweep + static guard (PR #777, `e87279fc`, Lane C) closes the window. Executed by a dispatch-executor subagent (claude-fable-5): fresh Phase 0 grep found 25 sites/13 files (the banked "~24" estimate +1); 24 TEXT sites swept, `dark:bg-primary-dark` D6 button-backgrounds count-verified unchanged (114→114). The FU's own falsification clause fired once — `NewAgentEmptyState.jsx:45`'s white CTA on a teal hero passes with the un-lifted color and would fail with the lifted one, so it was kept and allowlisted rather than swept. A new repo-wide static guard (deliberately not an extension of the `.glass.hero`-scoped hero guard, per the brief's carve-out) locks the pattern out at authoring time, with its Rule 23 falsifier demonstrated red twice. The orchestrator independently re-verified CI, scope, and dispositions, then **held this PR at PR-open** rather than auto-merging — two items exceeded its conditional-merge grant: the NewAgentEmptyState deviation from the brief's literal instruction, and two financing sites certified by contrast-math instead of their (fixture-heavy, unavailable) runtime axe smokes. The operator reviewed both and merged. **Window summary:** Lane A (PR #775, financing display polish) auto-merged clean; Lane B (PR #776, cat08 dossier setTheme conformance) merged on an operator-granted Rule 13 waiver (TA-tier verified only, Agent/UM/BM blocked by the pre-existing banked `*@agencytrack.test` credential gap); Lane C merged on operator review of two orchestrator-surfaced judgment calls. All three post-merge fills complete; no lanes remain open. **Deferred-verification FUs carried forward:** full cat08 dossier re-run once seeding unblocks the other 3 role tiers; the stale BM GoalsPanel nav helper in `contrast-debt-axe-surfaces.mjs`.

**Prior — Financing display polish — SHIPPED (PR #775, `507c812c`, 2026-07-02, Lane A of the orchestrated window).** Three banked display/error-posture fixes across the two financing surfaces, executed by a dispatch-executor subagent (claude-fable-5) and auto-merged by the orchestrator after independent 7-gate verification (CI green on final HEAD `3712444d`, head-SHA match, scope-clean 8-file diff, all 9 bot comments dispositioned with the one Major implemented, smokes value-level green with 0 orphans). FIX 1 kills the "−0%" render in `formatAdjustmentPct`; FIX 2 aligns the K10a roster term chip to the 1-based `financingMonthIndex` convention (chip now agrees with the K9 self-view for the same effectiveDate); FIX 3 replaces the K9 recon candidate-year probe loop with the manager panel's ledger-derived single year and narrows the load-bearing `.catch` to `permission-denied` only — genuine read failures now surface as the error state instead of silently rendering "no recon" (Rule 23 falsifier red-verified). This closes the "Track K · K10a → two post-merge CodeRabbit findings" FU in FOLLOW_UPS.md and the #767 known-gaps recon-year item. **Orchestrated window continues:** Lane B (cat08 dossier setTheme conformance, XS) and Lane C (repo-wide `dark:text-primary-dark` text sweep + static guard extension, M) run serially next; each holds at PR-open for orchestrator gate-verification before any merge. *(Archived to history when PR #785 `95529a75` was added as the 4th "Where we left off" entry on 2026-07-03.)*

**Prior — K7 termination-risk monitor + clause-5.3 notify duty — SHIPPED — Phase 6 CF verification 10/10 PASS (PR #762, `8dc91d1e`, 2026-06-26).** FLAG ONLY (no `LEGAL_TRANSITIONS` change). `financingMissEngine.js`, `notifyFinancingAdjustment` CF (BM+-gated, transport reuse, NOT `sendComplianceNudge`), `FinancingRiskPanel.jsx` (6th FinancingTab subview). K7 ships ZERO `firestore.rules` changes — existing wildcard `config/{docId}` covers the config doc; Phase 6 = `firebase deploy --only functions` only. NEGATIVE legal-integrity proof: fabricated `adjustmentPct:0.99` on ≤10% ledger → CF returns `condition-not-met`, ZERO artifacts. *(Archived to history when K10a PR #769 `ffb4789a` was added as the 4th "Where we left off" entry on 2026-07-01.)*

**Prior — K5 validation-schedule proration SHIPPED (PR #754, `12a026c`, 2026-06-25).** Pure `financingProration.js` (basis machine + K3 `computeApiChain` reuse + `computeAdjustmentPct`), `setFinancingProration` forward-create + merge, `FinancingProrationPanel.jsx`, rules restructure. Phase 6 seeded write-read-verify 9/9 PASS against live rules. *(Archived to history when K7 cleanup PR #764 `fa5854a8` was added as the 4th "Where we left off" entry on 2026-06-26.)*

## Recently-shipped archive — PR #792 (archived 2026-07-05, Rule 16 5-row cap)

| [#792](https://github.com/Kelsean868/agencytrack/pull/792) | `41935ef8` | **fix(goals): reconcile MDRT thresholds + AwardsReachPanel merged accessor + spec correction** (HUMAN-MERGE — agent-facing numbers + PDF surface; **no rules/schema/functions, no deploy**). Closure sweep on audit-only recon (2026-07-04) that found Goals v3.1–3.3 (#638/#641/#643/#645) already SHIPPED, contradicting `docs/goals-v3-spec.md`'s stale "PLANNED" status. **Phase 0 falsification gate caught a brief-premise shift (dispatcher-authorized Rule 9 extension):** `AwardsReachPanel.jsx` had zero direct ruleset import — it fell through to `awardsEngine.js`'s own `ruleset = DEFAULT_RULESET_2026` default parameter, and the cited pattern to mirror (`AgentAwardsPanel`) has no async fetch of its own — it receives an already-resolved `ruleset` prop from `AgentDashboard.jsx`'s existing `getMergedAwardsRuleset` fetch. Corrected fix: `AwardsReachPanel.jsx` accepts a `ruleset` prop (thread as `computeAgentAwards`'s 5th arg, mirroring `AgentAwardsPanel` exactly — no new fetch/loading/error state); `AgentDashboard.jsx` mount gets `ruleset={awardsRuleset}` (one line, reusing already-fetched state). **MDRT reconcile:** migrated `AgentReportDocument.jsx` + `HeroCard.jsx` (+ `HeroCard.test.jsx` fixtures) from the legacy flat `MDRT_THRESHOLD` (500,000, `src/constants/mdrt.js`, deleted) to the three-tier `MDRT_THRESHOLDS_2026.mdrt` (688,800, `src/config/mdrtThresholds/2026.js`) already used by `MdrtTracker.jsx` — zero remaining repo-wide references confirmed via ripgrep. **Spec correction:** `docs/goals-v3-spec.md` status → SHIPPED with PR numbers; flat-math deviation from the "reuse Commission Playground blended-rate math" intent banked as an OPEN item gated on a Phase 5 recon rider. **Phase 5 recon rider verdict: PARTIAL** — per-policy `proposedFrequency` data exists (`policiesService.js`, `VALID_FREQUENCIES={'A','S','Q','M'}`) but only for Policy-Ledger-adopting agents (`usesPolicyLedger` flag, not universal); weekly submissions carry no mode/frequency field; Commission Playground's `modeMix` is not persisted anywhere and its one production call site (`CommissionAnchorStrip.jsx`) always falls back to 100%-annual `DEFAULT_MODE_MIX`. Decision on real blended-math migration NOT made in this PR — banked as an FU for CD + Kyron. **Also banked (not fixed, by dispatcher direction):** `GoalsPanel.jsx`'s manager-side `AwardsReachPanel` mount has the identical missing-`ruleset` gap, but that host never fetches a merged ruleset at all — left unchanged rather than introducing a new fetch pattern outside this PR's scope. lint 0; suite 4202/4202 (+4 new: 2 `AwardsReachPanel` ruleset-prop parity/override tests); build clean. **Known gap (Rule 22):** the `awardsEngine.js` default-parameter fallback (`ruleset = DEFAULT_RULESET_2026`) remains in place by design — any FUTURE mount that forgets the `ruleset` prop silently reverts to DEFAULT with no warning; no live tenant currently overrides `awardsRuleset` in Firestore, so the merged-accessor fix is proven only by unit tests, not a production data path. |

### Where-we-left-off archive — Runs 5-7 promotion #858 (archived 2026-07-26, Rule 16 3-entry cap)

**Runs 5-7 promoted staging → production (PR #858 merge `10670bd7`, 2026-07-15).** Fable Run 5 (Company Config slice 1: diff-only config substrate — `configService`/`configAuditService`/`ConfigProvider`+`useConfig`; 46-item real-defaults registry with 36 parity tests; the 12-section registry-driven tenant-admin surface with five-state row grammar, ⌘F palette, history drawer, draft/save lifecycle, mobile drill-in; live-wired Activity Standards + Feature Flags + Awards & Clubs; `configAudit` append-only + `featureFlags` allowlist rules) plus a Run 5 rulings fast-follow (override-count indicator, palette-value honesty) plus Run 6 (Organization-section crash fix, campaign persistency-gate registry rows, two new VH legs) plus Run 7 (full Tier-0 systemic sweep — four-states/swallow-disposition incl. the dead-error-card reconnect, focus-trap verify, dense-table, motion; **SEC-012** kiosk degraded-names fix — real names sourced from kiosk-readable submission docs, zero new read surface, no rules widening; WizardForm draft-load silent-overwrite guard; week-rollover seeder residue bug fix) shipped as an 82-commit merge commit, history preserved, no squash. Backend delta was **rules-only** (three additive arms, flag-reviewed safe): `configAudit` create-only collection; `featureFlags` diff-scoped allowlist guard (empty-diff passes); `managerActivityStandardOverrides` read-only list arm (TA/PA). No index or functions changes this cycle. Old-frontend config-write health check passed. Prod verification passed on `portal.agencytrack.app`: all 12 Company Config sections render without an error boundary, feature flags still fail-closed, kiosk shows real agent names, consoles clean throughout.

**Verification mishap this promotion (bank the lesson, don't repeat it):** the first prod-verification pass targeted `https://agencytrack.vercel.app` — a non-canonical alias, not the locked App-host invariant (`portal.agencytrack.app`, CONTEXT.md § App host) — and produced false crash reports that triggered a rollback scare before the wrong-URL mistake was caught and corrected. Every check then passed cleanly against the real canonical host. Banked as a HIGH follow-up (FOLLOW_UPS.md § Prod-verification tooling) to hard-pin `portal.agencytrack.app` and reject `*.vercel.app` in verification tooling going forward.

Four new guards: no-twin-lists, rules-mirror (set equality), `TYPE_TONE` byte-parity, and a table contract test pinning every semantic axis.

### `Last updated` — entry dropped per the 3-entry cap

**Prior:** **v3 P0-B landed on `staging` (2026-07-31, PR #884 squash `ab9b3132`) — NOT on `main`; `Current main HEAD` is unchanged and this reaches production at the next promotion.** New pure module `src/lib/activityLedger.js` (`pcBreakdown` / `attributeCalls` / `loggedFor` / `declaredFor` / `weekTotals`) — the activity ledger's entire arithmetic, with **zero** Firestore, React, service or component imports, so **nothing to deploy**. Proven by three mutation-verified property families at `numRuns` 200 / seed `20260730`: **partition** (attribution — which block claims which call), **monotonicity** (direction — a new record never lowers a figure), **aggregation** (magnitude — per-block `max(dials, itemised)`, never the sum). Calls are pooled into ONE `CALLS` row rather than split PC/SC, because a call record carries no type and adding a block re-types existing calls, making any per-type split non-monotonic. Two supporting leaves: `src/constants/appointmentStatus.js` (new — the status vocabulary, previously split between `plannerService`'s enum and `planner.helpers`' sets) and `COUNTED_LIVE_CODES` / `CALL_ATTRIBUTED_CODES` / `callAttributed` added to `ACTIVITY_METADATA`; `buildWeekDates` lifted to `src/lib/schema/dailyActivity` (removing an import edge, not adding one). `fast-check` added as a devDependency. Also corrected `03-DATA-MODEL.md:30` — `SC` is "Seen call" = **contact made**, NOT a service call — and banked a prototype-defect register in FOLLOW_UPS plus the mutation-verification rule in `CLAUDE.md`.

### Where we left off — entry 3 (2026-08-14, flake investigation closed)

**PRIOR — 2026-08-14. The flake investigation is closed and the unit-test gate is trustworthy again.** That is the headline for anyone resuming: for six rounds a red CI on this repo did not reliably distinguish a regression from scheduling noise, and it now does. Phase 0 (#898 `5db36a12`) falsified the awaiting-pattern hypothesis — the dispositive evidence was the CONTROL rather than any burn, since `CompliancePanel.nudge` carried the PROXY shape through both its 57% era and its 0/200 era — and built an instrument that observes the real mechanism, a stale keydown-handler closure, on every iteration. Phase 1 (#899 `90a7718b`) applied the fix and proved it **by trace, not by rate**: 300 iterations, 4350 trace rows, zero stale-served dispatches, zero `k>=2`, zero failures, with a mutation control showing the failures return when the flush is removed. It adds a new shared module, `src/test-utils/flushPendingEffects.js`, and a CLAUDE.md rule for choosing between `userEvent` and `fireEvent`. #900 (`c06fff0d`) filed the two loose ends.

**What is NOT done, stated plainly so the run-rate does not imply otherwise.** The **production** defect at `MeetingMode.jsx:929-949` is filed and not fixed — the test fix masks that timing window rather than closing it, and whoever owns the component fix must add a deliberate regression test or we will have deleted the only detector and kept the bug. **P0-E was stood down unshipped** (runner-level remedies are closed permanently, not deferred). **P0-F is not started**, and it carries the first executed smoke over the v3 write path — so nothing in Phase 0 has been exercised against a live write. **There are no v3 screens**: Phase 0 is vocabulary, ledger arithmetic, a commit seam and CI gates. One measurement is still owed on the `AgentPlannerPanel` double-serving traces (count `document` keydown listeners on the unwrapped component: >1 = real leaked listener, exactly 1 = instrument artifact).

**The blocker before any backend-touching track is unchanged and getting worse:** the secondary-reviewer decision (FOLLOW_UPS § External code reviewer, HIGH). CodeRabbit has now been rate-limited four consecutive cycles, including on #899's FINAL HEAD — where the +11/-1 fix for its own finding merged *unreviewed-because-rate-limited* while the checks UI displayed the same green tick as reviewed-and-clean. Gemini has been absent since its 2026-07-17 sunset. **Everything above is on `staging` only; `Current main HEAD` is unchanged and none of it is in production.**


**Run A shipped to production (PR #874 merge `219cf324`, 2026-07-26, 17 commits, staging→main).** Three build tiers: Tier 1 (#865 `4e7a287b`, hygiene + verification substrate), Tier 2 (#866 `f22c57f8`, Planner v2 E1–E5 — desktop board, drag-drop reschedule wired to the existing `postponeWithRebook`, per-appointment notes thread, running-late cascade, collapsed-rail space adaptation), Tier 3 (#870 `8a1a17e4`, Track J conformance closeout — R-08 ranked-podium verified, R-06 saved-scenario chips + a daily cadence chip built, 2 evidenced STOPs on rules-gated items). Three interim fix PRs landed between Tiers 2 and 3 from post-merge staging-smoke fallout, all diagnosed and closed the same session: #867 (`c02c7386` — 2 real bugs found by the smokes: sidebar pin-star intercepting nav clicks + the desktop board's span load-range, plus smoke presence-check hardening), #868 (`9b7f8b7f` — 3 residual planner-smoke failures, all residue/precondition, **no product defects**), #869 (`c20210e8` — re-diagnosed A3 as a Saturday-only fixture-band collision, **no product defect**; the prior residue hypothesis for it was wrong). **Backend delta across the entire range (Track K P1 through Run A) is ZERO** — verified via `git diff` on `firestore.rules` / `firestore.indexes.json` / `functions/` from `2368648b` to `219cf324`: no hits. Client/test/docs only; nothing to deploy.

**Corrections landed as part of this fill, not as new work:** FOLLOW_UPS.md's § BIG ONE entry — banked 2026-07-17, claiming the E1–E5 planner-completion track was "UNBUILT" — is now marked RESOLVED. Verified directly against source at `219cf324` (Rule 17: `src/components/planner/PlannerDesktopBoard.jsx`, `RunningLateSheet.jsx`, `NotesThread.jsx` all exist and match the spec), not assumed from the PR titles. The Run 9 entry immediately below this one repeats the same now-stale "remains entirely unbuilt" claim — left in place as a historical record of what was true on 2026-07-13, with a pointer added rather than rewritten. **Lesson banked:** an entry that asserts absence is the kind most likely to rot silently, since nothing about building the feature forces an edit to the doc that claims it doesn't exist.

### Last updated — entry (P0-G block, dropped when #912 became the new current)

**v3 P0-G landed on `staging` (2026-08-16, PR #904 squash `5f7dd06b`) — NOT on `main`; `Current main HEAD` is unchanged and this reaches production at the next promotion.** **This is a SEVENTH Phase 0 item, added after Phase 0 was declared closed** — say so plainly rather than pretend the phase was always seven. It exists because **ruling D3 landed afterwards and invalidated a stated invariant in P0-B**: `activityLedger.js` documented *"Calls carry no status — a logged call happened"*, and D3 (dialer is `tel:` plus a captured outcome, **no telephony bridge**) made the outcome vocabulary a Phase 0 concern the moment there was no bridge to supply it. New pure module `src/lib/schema/callRecord.js`; **nothing to deploy** (leaf, zero Firestore/React/service/component imports). The model is **two DISJOINT lanes** — `evidencedContacts` (a machine confirmed it) and `declaredOnlyContacts` (the agent's word, nothing has confirmed it yet) — with `total` their sum, honest because no call sits in both. **The property is CONSERVATION:** an agreeing verification is a **TRANSFER, not an addition** (`total` unchanged, Δevidenced = −ΔdeclaredOnly), strictly stronger than "no derived count decreases" — the weaker form passes a double-count cleanly, because a double-count makes `total` *rise*. **A contradiction removes the confirmation, never the claim**, and moves no credit in either direction; `contestedContacts` surfaces it for a human. ⚠ **The contradiction property is a PLACEHOLDER, not doctrine** — it holds only while verification-vs-disposition is undecided, and a ruling either way retires it; that ruling is the dispatcher's. `pcBreakdown` is untouched and must stay so: a dial counts identically whatever its disposition. 21 tests at `numRuns` 200 / seed `20260812`, 398 ms added; **the first mutation PASSED**, exposing a generator too dilute to reach its own subject — the finding, not the mutation, is what survives.

### Active track — entry (P0-G-seventh-item block, dropped when #912 became the new current)

**v3 Phase 0 has a SEVENTH item, and it was added AFTER the phase was declared closed. P0-G is DONE on `staging`** (#904 `5f7dd06b`, 2026-08-16). State the irregularity rather than smooth it over: Phase 0 was closed at P0-D, then **ruling D3 landed and invalidated a stated invariant in P0-B** — `activityLedger.js` documented *"Calls carry no status — a logged call happened"*, which stopped being true the moment D3 settled the dialer as `tel:` plus a **captured outcome** with **no telephony bridge**. The outcome vocabulary therefore became a Phase 0 concern, and P0-G defines it. Landed on `staging`, none on `main`: **P0-A** `0e4a6bfb` (#882) · **P0-B** `ab9b3132` (#884) · **P0-C** `a3b57b35` (#892) · **P0-D** `a55329ba` (#893) · **P0-G** `5f7dd06b` (#904). **⚠ WHAT PHASE 0 STILL HAS NOT DELIVERED, unchanged by P0-G: (1) P0-E was STOOD DOWN UNSHIPPED** — its `maxForks` capping was falsified within an hour (#889 closed unmerged; #888/#890 scratch recon, also closed) and the isolation result closed runner-level remedies **permanently**. **(2) P0-F is NOT STARTED** — task↔event unification, which carries the **first executed smoke** over the v3 write path. P0-G explicitly did not change this: it has **no write path and no smoke**, deliberately, because `src/actions/` is still contract-only and `logCallOutcome` has no implementation. **(3) There are STILL NO SCREENS.** Phase 0 is vocabulary, ledger arithmetic, a commit seam, CI gates and now a call-evidence model — no user-facing v3 surface exists. **Next in sequence:** P0-F, then Phase 2.1 (floors + the ledger UI; owns the `contacts` field on the `CALLS` row per ruling 5c, which now reads its vocabulary from `callRecord.js` rather than inventing one, plus consolidating the `WEEK_COUNTER_ROWS`/`COUNTER_ROWS` pair P0-A allowlisted). **Two open questions P0-G deliberately did NOT answer, both dispatcher's to rule:** verification-vs-disposition when they contradict (a placeholder property holds the dispute open and is retired by a ruling either way), and verification-vs-verification when a second source disagrees with the first (`applyVerification` is add-only, which is declining to decide, not deciding). **Decision gate before any backend-touching track: the secondary-reviewer question** (FOLLOW_UPS § External code reviewer, HIGH) — on #904 CodeRabbit was skip-by-default (OSS repo) and reviewed only on explicit request, **rate-limited to one review per rolling hour**, and Gemini is now **structurally absent** (sunset 2026-07-17), so the second reviewer slot is effectively empty rather than merely quiet.

### Where we left off — entry (P0-G block, rotated at the 3-block cap)

**PRIOR — 2026-08-16. Phase 0 gained a seventh item after it had been declared closed, and the reason is worth stating plainly rather than smoothing over.** P0-G (#904 `5f7dd06b`, **`staging` only — `main` has not moved**) exists because **ruling D3 landed after Phase 0 closed and invalidated a stated invariant in P0-B**. `activityLedger.js` had documented *"Calls carry no status — a logged call happened"* and deferred the disposition vocabulary to Phase 1.5; D3 settled the dialer as `tel:` plus a **captured outcome with no telephony bridge**, which made that vocabulary a Phase 0 concern the moment there was nothing else to supply it. Both docblocks are corrected in-source rather than left to mislead the next reader.

**What P0-G actually is, for anyone resuming.** A pure leaf module `src/lib/schema/callRecord.js` — zero Firestore, React, service or component imports, so **nothing to deploy**. Two **DISJOINT** lanes: `evidencedContacts` (a machine confirmed it) and `declaredOnlyContacts` (the agent's word, nothing has confirmed it yet), with `total` their sum — honest precisely because no call sits in both. The declared field carries `Only` in its name deliberately: `declaredContacts` invites "all the contacts the agent claimed", which is a larger quantity, and a misread manager-facing figure is the failure this module exists to prevent. **The governing property is CONSERVATION, not monotonicity** — an agreeing verification is a **TRANSFER, not an addition** (`total` unchanged, Δevidenced = −ΔdeclaredOnly). That is strictly stronger than "no derived count decreases", which passes a double-count cleanly because a double-count makes `total` *rise*. `pcBreakdown` is untouched and must stay so: **a dial counts identically whatever its disposition**, and that blindness is what lets contacts be additive instead of re-opening arithmetic that took two rounds to get right.

**Two things here are placeholders, NOT doctrine — do not let them harden.** (1) When a verification **contradicts** the agent's disposition, no credit moves in either direction and `contestedContacts` flags it; that property **holds only while verification-vs-disposition is undecided, and a ruling either way retires it — the ruling is the dispatcher's.** (2) When a *second source* disagrees with the first, `applyVerification` is **add-only** and returns the call unchanged; that is declining to decide, not deciding. Both are written into the source so they cannot quietly become rules nobody chose.

**Two process findings from this cycle that outlive the PR.** First: **the first mutation run PASSED.** Mutating the declared count and re-running the monotonicity property caught nothing — the generator spread calls over seven days and sampled one, diluting the case that mattered to roughly 1 run in 90. The fix (dense same-day generator, assertions across all seven days) is the same lesson `activityLedger.test.js` had already banked and that had not been applied. **A property that has never failed may not be wired to anything**, and the only way to know is to break it on purpose. Second: **CodeRabbit reasoned correctly from a wrong docblock and produced a confident wrong fix** — it proposed gating both lanes on non-contradiction, which would make `total` fall 1→0 the moment a machine disagreed with an agent. Its premise, "the v3 contract that contested calls count in neither lane", traced back to **one incorrect comment I had written**, and nothing else. The defence was checking where the premise came from rather than accepting the inference built on it.

**What is NOT done, unchanged by this slice.** **P0-F is still not started** — task↔event unification, which carries the **first executed smoke** over the v3 write path. P0-G explicitly did not change that: it has **no write path and no smoke**, deliberately, because `src/actions/` remains contract-only and `logCallOutcome` has no implementation. **There are still NO SCREENS.** And the **secondary-reviewer gate is worse, not better**: on #904 CodeRabbit was skip-by-default (OSS repo), reviewed only on explicit request, and is **rate-limited to one review per rolling hour**; Gemini is now **structurally absent** (sunset 2026-07-17), so the second slot is empty rather than quiet.

Remedy follows a new CLAUDE.md rule — a test modelling a USER pressing a key takes `userEvent.keyboard`; a test dispatching at a SPECIFIC target on purpose (form-field bail, focus trap) keeps `fireEvent` and takes the flush separately. 30 call sites gain `await` as the mechanical consequence. **Acceptance: 300 iterations · 4350 trace rows · 0 stale-served · 0 `k≥2` · 0 failures**, plus a mutation control (flush removed, awaits kept → failures RETURN at 3/100, so `act()` is the active ingredient) and a live re-verification of the form-field negative control. **What this means for a reader resuming here: the unit-test gate can now be trusted** — a red is again evidence of a regression rather than scheduling noise. ⚠ The **production** defect at `MeetingMode.jsx:929-949` is FILED, NOT FIXED: the test fix masks the window rather than closing it (FOLLOW_UPS § production defect). |

**What `main` received.** v3 Phase 0 **P0-A / P0-B / P0-C / P0-D / P0-G**, the **#899 flake fix** (`src/test-utils/flushPendingEffects.js`), and the **#907 governance convergence** — `CLAUDE.md`'s router structure with its `docs/agents/*` detail files, the union of `CONTEXT.md` / `FOLLOW_UPS.md` / `CONTEXT-history.md`, and the corrected branch-protection claim. 98 files.

Production already carried the 16-type allowlist, because **#878 was itself DEPLOY-GATED and was deployed when it landed**. There was no production gap at any point.

Two lessons recorded from that audit rather than the (absent) incident: **per-slice "zero backend delta" claims do not compose across a range** — every Phase 0 slice truthfully claimed none, and #878 sat in the same range without being a Phase 0 slice — and **git cannot answer "is production stale"**; only the deploy target can. The trigger-vs-verdict runbook step is banked in `docs/agents/release-and-post-merge.md`.

Exists because **ruling D3** (`tel:` + a captured outcome, no telephony bridge) falsified an invariant P0-B had written down: *"Calls carry no status — a logged call happened."* **Disjoint lanes:** `evidencedContacts` (a machine confirmed it) · `declaredOnlyContacts` (the agent's word, nothing has confirmed it yet) · `total` = their sum, honest because no call is in both. The declared field is named `declaredOnly` deliberately — `declaredContacts` invites "all the contacts the agent claimed", a larger quantity, and a misread manager-facing figure is the failure this module exists to prevent. **The property is CONSERVATION, not monotonicity:** an agreeing verification is a **TRANSFER, not an addition** — `total` unchanged, Δevidenced = −ΔdeclaredOnly. Strictly stronger than "no count went down", which passes a double-count cleanly because a double-count makes `total` *rise*. **A contradiction removes the confirmation, never the claim:** agent claims / machine denies → stays in `declaredOnly`, never promoted; machine claims / agent did not → no lane, never credited. ### Last updated — entry (#909 block, dropped when #919 became the new current)

**PR #909 merged to `main` (squash `ee5444bd`, 2026-08-26) — Daily Capture call fields, `DAILY_ACTIVITY_VERSION` 1→2, NO migration.** Widens the daily schema so KQM Calls' two facts survive into the weekly report: which campaign a call belonged to, and whether a referral came out of it. **Additive split:** `dials` stays the authoritative daily total; a new optional `dialsByType {cold, referral, followUp, seminarTradeshow}` rides alongside it, so every pre-v2 daily doc still aggregates to byte-identical weekly numbers. **The aggregator fallback is PER ENTRY, not per week** — an entry with any non-zero bucket contributes its breakdown, one with none contributes its whole `dials` to `cold`; a per-week fallback would silently drop unsplit days in a mixed week. Points-neutral by construction, `fast-check`-verified at `numRuns` 200 / seed `20260826`, mutation-proven. **Ruling D-SC** closes a real defect (F3): the daily pace badge and the aggregated weekly draft disagreed on a week's score because `computeDayPoints` mapped `serviceContacts → serviceCalls` while the aggregator wrote no `serviceCalls` at all. Both now read one real daily `serviceCalls` field. ⚠ **Deliberate correction, not a regression:** a day carrying only `serviceContacts` now scores 0 for service, and there is NO UI to enter `serviceCalls` yet — the KQM ingest endpoint (not built here) is the intended writer, so service points are effectively 0 until it lands. **Ruling D-TD:** "Target Dials" is the 4-sum, read through `extractFields.totalTelAttempts`. **`aggregateDailyToWeekly` lives in `functions/` — merging ships the code, NOT the cron; `firebase deploy --only functions` is a separate dispatcher action (Rule 19) and was NOT done by this PR.** A new MEDIUM follow-up is banked: the Sunday aggregator now unconditionally zeroes agent-typed `serviceCalls`/`referralsObtained` on an unsubmitted draft once the functions deploy ships — mitigated by an omit-when-zero design, not yet fully closed.

`ee5444bd` (PR #909 squash — Daily Capture call fields, schema v1→v2, 2026-08-26). **MERGED *AND* DEPLOYED — `firebase deploy --only functions` ran 2026-08-26 07:35 TT.** `functions/aggregators/dailyToWeekly.js` now writes `serviceCalls`/`referralsObtained` from real daily fields — and that behaviour is now LIVE: `Deploy complete!` with `functions[aggregateDailyToWeekly(us-central1)] Successful update operation`, confirmed by `firebase functions:list` (v1, scheduled, us-central1, nodejs20). Merged and deployed remain independent states in general; here they are simply both true. **For the record, the first deploy attempt FAILED** with HTTP 403 `Write access to project 'agencytrack-2a610' was denied: please check billing account associated and retry` — the Cloud Billing account had to be reattached, and the retry succeeded with no code change.

Production is serving the #909 aggregator. A new MEDIUM follow-up rides with it — without mitigation the Sunday cron would zero two agent-typed weekly fields on unsubmitted drafts. The omit-when-zero guard shipped inside this same PR and is now live, so the data-loss path is closed; the residual (a genuine correction-to-zero cannot propagate once a daily writer exists) is NOT closed and is owned by whoever builds the KQM ingest endpoint.

### Where we left off — entry (#909 block, rotated at the 3-block cap)

**PRIOR — 2026-08-26. PR #909 merged to `main` — Daily Capture call fields, `DAILY_ACTIVITY_VERSION` 1→2.**

## Archived by the PR #924 post-merge fill (2026-08-27, Rule 16 caps)

- **PR #915 merged to `main` (squash `302b87a6`, 2026-08-26) — linked call sources, slice A: the link and its lifecycle.** New collection `tenants/{tid}/callSources/{sourceId}` — an external calling system presents a bearer token, the token resolves to a stored link, the link names the agent whose KPIs move; identity never travels in a request body, so a typo or tampered field cannot silently credit the wrong agent. **Slice A is the link and its lifecycle ONLY** (create, list, revoke, revoke-on-deactivation) — no ingest endpoint, nothing consumes the token yet. **Two independent, mutation-proven defences on the F4 trap:** the callables gate on `MANAGER_ROLES` (branch_manager and above), and a NEW `canManageCallSources()` rules helper — deliberately narrower than `canManage()`, which routes through `isManager()` and includes `unit_manager`. Substituting `canManage()` was tried on purpose (twice — once in the callable's role set, once in the rules) and both times caught by exactly the tests built for it going red, nothing else. Only the SHA-256 `tokenHash` is stored; the raw token is returned once at creation and never persisted, logged, or recoverable. `deactivateUser`'s `active === false` branch now revokes every inbound link crediting that user, batched — **one-way**, reactivation does not restore them. **A CodeRabbit pass (triggered explicitly per Ruling 19) found four real defects this PR introduced**, all fixed before merge: `canManageCallSources` admitted ANY `tenant_admin` cross-tenant (copied `canManage()`'s unscoped shape, wrong here because these docs are credentials — now only `platform_admin` is cross-tenant, mutation-verified); `createCallSource` accepted a deactivated `creditUid`, letting a manager re-link someone just offboarded; `revokeInboundLinks` committed one unbounded batch (a `serverTimestamp` is a field TRANSFORM costing a second operation against Firestore's 500-op cap — 251 links would have exceeded it and left the offboarded user's links live); and the tab was mounted only for `branch_manager`, so `sales_manager`/`tenant_admin`/`platform_admin` were permitted by the callable but had no way to reach the UI. **One residual was found and only partly closed:** `createCallSource`'s deactivation check has a TOCTOU window (a link created in the ~100ms between the user-deactivation read and write survives) — banked as a LOW follow-up rather than left unstated, closing it needs a transaction the brief doesn't scope. ⚠ **DEPLOYED 2026-08-26 18:25 UTC.** This PR modifies `deactivateUser`, an existing CF whose existing callers already exercise it — per CLAUDE.md's additive-carve-out rule that puts the rules+functions deploy at **post-merge only**. `firebase deploy --only functions,firestore:rules` has run (Rule 19, dispatcher action) — this is code-only until it does. **The staging smoke is now UNBLOCKED but still OUTSTANDING** - it was previously impossible because the callables existed in no Firebase project; it mints real credentials, so per the brief it stays staging-only. **A CI anomaly self-resolved before merge:** GitHub created no workflow run at all for two consecutive pushes (~15 minutes each) despite the workflow having no path filters — the same silent-no-run condition banked from v3 P0-D. Checks eventually reported SUCCESS on the final head; not investigated further. Brief: `docs/briefs/linked-call-sources-slice-a-kickoff.md`.

- `302b87a6` (PR #915 squash — linked call sources slice A, 2026-08-26). **MERGED *AND* DEPLOYED - `firebase deploy --only functions,firestore:rules` ran 2026-08-26 18:25 UTC.** `firestore: released rules firestore.rules to cloud.firestore`, plus `functions[createCallSource]` and `functions[revokeCallSource]` Successful create operation and `functions[deactivateUser]` Successful update operation; `firebase functions:list` confirms all three live (v1, callable, us-central1). **The first attempt FAILED** with `Error: Failed to list functions for agencytrack-2a610` - a transient GCP API read error. It was verified to have released NOTHING (rules reached `compiled successfully` with no `released` line; `createCallSource` confirmed absent from production) before the retry, so there was never a half-deployed state. Touches `firestore.rules` (+36 — new `callSources` match block + `canManageCallSources` helper) and `functions/` (3 new callables under `functions/callSources/`, plus a hook added to the existing `deactivateUser`). Modifying an existing CF whose callers already exercise it put this at post-merge-only per the additive-carve-out rule — `firebase deploy --only functions,firestore:rules` was run as that dispatcher action after the merge. Until it runs: `createCallSource`/`revokeCallSource` do not exist in any Firebase project, the new rules block is not enforced anywhere, and `deactivateUser`'s existing behavior for existing callers is unaffected — the new revoke-hook code ships inert.

Until it runs, the staging smoke (mints real credentials, so staging-only per the brief) cannot run either, because the callables don't exist in any Firebase project. **What comes after, per the brief (not this slice, context only):** B — the `ingestCallActivity` endpoint; C — the KQM Calls webhook; D — Daily Capture steppers for `serviceCalls`/`referralsObtained`. **CORRECTED by slice B:** the omit-when-zero retirement was sequenced after C, and that was wrong — the trigger condition is a DAILY WRITER, and B is the daily writer.
