A typed `0` answers a field; a blank does not. Why: a `0` nobody entered is indistinguishable on the saved doc, and on every later report, from a `0` somebody checked - `savePersistency` already rejected a missing input on 24-month months for exactly this reason, so the form now agrees with the service instead of handing it four zeros that would pass. An existing manual entry always wins, for the derived three as well. New `src/lib/persistency/ledgerPrefill.js` (pure), `AnnuityRuleSwitch.jsx`, `CountedPoliciesDrawer.jsx` (counted policies + the pending death claim + at-risk annuities in THREE SEPARATE sections - not summed, because blending them adds a container to its contents). `savePersistency` gains an OPTIONAL provenance arg (`manualConfirmedBy`/`At`, `ledgerDerived`, `ledgerExportDate`, `annuityMissedPremiumRule`) - additive, absent on every existing doc, nothing branches on it; the rule is stored because the same ledger gives **86.6% under `ignore` and 72.2% under `lapse`**, so a percentage without its rule cannot be reproduced. Two operator fixes landed before merge: the lock banner said "your figures override THEIR self-entry" to an agent entering her own month (now "Saving locks September. You cannot edit it after saving.", keyed on `writerUid === agentUid` so a producing manager on her own month gets it too), and the main card now shows "From portfolio import, 15 Sep 2026: 86.6% (not saved yet)" under "No record entered", vanishing once a record is saved. **A `toLocaleString` bug was caught in test: `en-GB` renders September as "Sept", not "Sep", and the spelling varies by ICU version** - replaced with a fixed month table so a money-surface label cannot drift between machines. 22 + 4 + 5 new tests; eslint clean; build green; preview smoke 7/7 both themes. **All six operator UI checks confirmed yes on the preview** (label, both switch positions, drawer sections, save block, console clean). **No `firestore.rules`, no `functions/`.** |

### Archived from CONTEXT.md on 2026-09-23 (hero-ledger H1 cycle - `Where we left off` cap)

**PRIOR - 2026-09-16. The OIPA portfolio import track P0-P3 shipped, and the LIVE import ran with the operator present. Eight PRs merged: #943 `a866fc87`, #944 `ba3b6abd`, #945 `b4662d51`, #946 `b9f402ed`, #947 `b2cf2a51`, #948 `0b8609b2`, #949 `bc2e58cc`, #950 `28974732`. MERGED; NO DEPLOY-GATED SURFACE ANYWHERE IN THE RANGE - QUERIED, not inferred.**

The goal in one line: Kyron uploads his OIPA/INGENIUM portfolio export, AgencyTrack fills his policy ledger from it, and the persistency tab works out which policies count for a report month instead of him typing seven totals. That is now true in production for September 2026.

- **P0 (#943/#944) - the pure parser.** 283 data rows -> 49 shadow rows dropped -> 5 test records skipped -> **229 docs**. All 11 of the brief's expected numbers matched exactly on the first run, nothing tuned. The one real finding was a precedence trap the brief's own counts settled: `NL` appears twice in the status map, and `denied 3` AND `settled+terminal 18` are both true only if the Policy Status check runs BEFORE the sub-status lookup. Dates are read by LOCAL parts rather than `toISOString()`, because a spreadsheet builds a cell at local midnight and UTC parts would shift the day backwards east of UTC - enough to push a policy issued on the 1st out of the 24-month window.
- **P1 (#945) - the derivation.** All five rows of the brief's expected table matched first time: 86.6% on the default `ignore` annuity rule, 72.2% on `lapse`, from 31 counted policies. The setting was renamed to `persistency.annuityMissedPremiumRule` with values `ignore`/`lapse` because `model.js` already uses `tatil24`/`legacy12` for the persistency MODEL - a different axis, and two meanings of one token on the same document would be read wrong by somebody.
- **P2 (#947) + P2b (#948) - the importer, then the filters, in that order because the operator ruled filters-first.** That ruling is the reason the CRO Delivery Register never showed 117 historical policies as awaiting delivery. P2b's new source-tree guard test immediately found a call site the P2 enumeration had missed: `CampaignLensPanel` was receiving the UNFILTERED ledger array while computing campaign eligibility, so an imported historical book would have earned campaign credit retroactively, silently.
- **P3 (#949) - the tab reads the ledger.** Three inputs derived and tagged; the four the export cannot supply left EMPTY, never 0, with the save blocked until each is answered. A `0` nobody entered is indistinguishable, on the saved document and every later report, from a `0` somebody checked.

**The live import ran in the operator's ordered sequence and every step passed:** 229 creates; a full rollback proving 229 docs and 229 history subdocs deleted with **0 orphans** (checked against doc ids captured BEFORE the delete - Firestore does not cascade, so a parent removed without its subcollection leaves history nothing can ever reach); a re-import of 229; the idempotency dry run reporting **0 creates / 229 unchanged**, which had been unit-tested only until that moment; the CRO register query returning **117 raw settled -> 0 after the filter**; and the live-ledger derivation reproducing **86.6% / 31 counted** exactly.

**Four premise corrections, all verified against source rather than assumed:** `firestore.rules` does NOT validate `policyClass` values at all, so `critical_illness` needed no rules change and no deploy; the class enum has three copies, not one; the brief's field inventory was missing `createdAt`, without which all 229 docs would have been silently invisible to every `orderBy('createdAt')` read query; and the campaign-lens gap above. Two mistakes of mine are also on the record in the PRs: I recommended running the organic-policy test as `A11Y_AGENT` on the premise that the CRO query is tenant-wide, which is only true WITHIN one tenant - that account lives in `tatillife_smoke` - and I briefly reported a policy as "never saved" when it had been saved to that other tenant, which `db.collection('tenants').get()` does not even list because it has subcollections but no tenant document.

**What is next, and what gates it:** **P4, the browser upload screen, is NOT started and is not authorised.** Two things gate it. `xlsx@0.18.5` is a root devDependency for the admin script ONLY and must not parse agent-picked files in a browser - banked HIGH, with `exceljs` or a server-side parse as the options. And P4 is the named revisit trigger for the one accepted gap: organic and imported policies are never tested together in a single tenant, because the only credentialled agent account is in `tatillife_smoke` and the operator ruled against creating a test agent in `tatillife_south` (it would show in manager rosters and leaderboards; `isTestAccount` hides an account from the leaderboards but not from rosters). Both halves are proven separately, and a second real agent onboarded to the tenant closes the gap for free. **Track J R2 remains UNBLOCKED and ready to dispatch, untouched this cycle.**

BM gets no rank pill, per the brief (a branch has no rank inside itself).

Confirmed the rank pill renders for UM (5 hero numerals: 4 aggregate stats + the pill) and is absent for BM (4 numerals), exactly as designed. Both roles default to the sidebar's "My Work" workspace - the smoke switches to "My Team" before navigating, and at the rail breakpoint clicks the containing `button.sidebar-link` rather than the visually-hidden label span (the sidebar forces a 72px icon-only rail at that width). CodeRabbit rate-limited (Free-plan quota) - no findings to disposition; Gemini absent per documented retired-reviewer policy.

Do not cut R2's branch until that ruling arrives. **Unchanged and still next on the other track:** life-pipeline slice 1B - the four custody dates and rules Arm F.

**PRIOR - 2026-09-08. PR #941 merged to `main` (squash `fb0416c8`) - Persistency P5: an adopted orphan lifts the numerator, not the denominator. MERGED; NO DEPLOY-GATED SURFACE.**

The rule, from the operator: the persistency denominator is built from the WRITING agent's business only. An agent who adopts an under-24-month orphan did not write that policy, so it never enters her gross; if she gets it reinstated, that value lands in her numerator, and net can legitimately exceed gross. `calculations.js` previously treated an adopted orphan exactly like new business she wrote - the same amount on both sides of the fraction, which can only pull toward 100%, never past it - and wired the orphan shortfall (`noNeeded`) to the new-business solution. On the brief's own worked example this told an agent to adopt 50,000 of orphan API when the truthful figure is 5,000, ten times overstated.

- **Fixed behind a tenant setting, not a hardcoded flip.** `orphansEnterDenominator` defaults to `false` (numerator-only, the rule above) and `true` restores the pre-P5 behaviour exactly - because persistency counting rules vary by carrier and this rule itself is stated by the operator but not yet confirmed in writing by Tatil. `calculations.js` stays pure; `PersistencyPlayground.jsx` resolves the boolean from config and passes it in.
- **The brief's storage plan did not survive Phase 1, and the dispatcher amended it mid-dispatch rather than letting the build proceed on a false premise.** The planned new `persistencySettings` config doc is absent from `ConfigProvider.HYDRATED_DOC_IDS` and would never load; the planned `companyConfigRegistry` entry assumed `mode: 'plain'` generalises beyond manager activity standards, and it does not - `baseValue()`, `saveAll()`, and the render branch are all hardcoded to that one item family, confirmed by reading each, not assumed. The amendment moved storage to the already-hydrated `companyMinimums` doc and dropped the registry entry and the UI-row deliverable entirely.
- **Consequence carried forward, not hidden:** the setting is runtime-real but has no UI - changing it in production today needs a direct Firestore write. Banked as a MEDIUM follow-up (Company Config has no generic plain-value surface) rather than fixed in this PR, to keep the slice off three shared admin-surface files (`ConfigProvider.jsx`, `useCompanyConfigState.js`, `CompanyConfigSurface.jsx`) the brief never named.
- **Three tests pinned the old maths - the brief named only one.** All three rewritten rather than deleted (`calculations.test.js:335`, `:488`, `:499`), plus the section-2 shortfall proof and the above-100% "Candice case" (110%, un-clipped) added as new tests. 383 files / 6,315 tests; eslint clean; build green.
- **The live smoke proves no regression, not the new arithmetic.** `newOrphansAdopted` has no UI lever yet and stays hardcoded to `0` in the Playground, so the corrected maths is reachable only through the pure functions and their tests until a lever exists. The standing P4 playground regression walk ran read-only against the immutable per-deployment preview URL (the branch-name alias exceeded the 63-char DNS limit) - 26/26 passed, both themes, no console errors. CodeRabbit reviewed with zero findings.
- **Note on this section:** #940 (Persistency P4/P4b/P4c) does not have its own entry above this one - the prior fill cycle updated the `Current state` table fields but not this prose section, so #939 was still shown as CURRENT here going into #941. Not corrected retroactively; #940's own content is intact in the `Current state` table and its own history-archive entries.

**What is next:** the rule itself is unconfirmed by Tatil - confirming it (via Victoria Walcott, alongside the Slice P3 process-document ask) remains an open operator action, and if the confirmation disagrees the fix is a one-value default flip. The Company Config generic-plain-value gap is queued as a follow-up, not a build item. **Unchanged and still next on the other track:** life-pipeline slice 1B - the four custody dates and rules Arm F.

## Archived from CONTEXT.md § Where we left off — 2026-09-23 fill (PR #969)

**PRIOR - 2026-09-20. Christmas Campaign 2026 slices C1 and C3 built unattended and merged. PRs #961 `f613c5a3` and #960 `adba6460`, on the Rev 2 brief landed at `cf153fc7`. MERGED; NO DEPLOY-GATED SURFACE - QUERIED across the whole range, not inferred.**

The goal in one line: the app tells the operator where he stands in the Christmas Campaign - cash, retreat room, and whether the persistency gate will pay him at all - from the policy ledger he already imported. Two of the four build slices are now true; the other two are authorised and not started.

**Why the track was rebriefed before it was built.** The campaign tracker did not exist at all - the Rev 1 brief was written on 3 Sept and never dispatched - and the gate that DID ship was a year out of date: `PERSISTENCY_GATE_BANDS` carried the PREVIOUS campaign's four bands (90/85/80/DQ), while the signed 2026 document has a cliff. The PDF was re-read end to end for Rev 2 (`Christmas Campaign and Retreat 2026 -- 02 Sept 2026 FINAL for Sales Force - Signed.pdf`, 9 pages, Garvis Ryan / Amery Rauseo / Anthony Shaw). Rule 5, page 3, has exactly two rows: `>= 90%` pays 100% of prize, `< 90%` is **Disqualified**. No partial band. Cash AND accommodation both. At 87% the shipped code would have told the operator he was on for half of TTD 70,000 and a room; the document says he gets nothing.

**The finding that reshaped the build, and it came from a query rather than a reading.** A read-only count against `tenants/tatillife_south/policies` returned **229 docs, every one `importSource: 'oipa_import'`, ZERO organic**. Five fall inside 1 Jul - 31 Dec 2026. PR #948's filter excludes imported docs from `CampaignLensPanel` so a historical book cannot earn campaign credit retroactively - correct intent, wrong mechanism, because it was hiding **100% of the operator's campaign production** and the lens rendered **TTD 0** against a real 3 apps / TTD 73,946.28. **C-D10 replaces origin with date: eligibility keys on `dateIssued` + status + `isSelfOrFamily`, never on `importSource`, for the campaign path and only there.** Origin was a proxy for age; the date is the thing itself, and it is also what Rule 2, Rule 7 and the Close of Business clause actually test. `excludeImported` is untouched everywhere else.

**Two bugs the slices turned up, neither found by review:** `Number(null)` is `0`, so a blank persistency month read as 0% and DISQUALIFIED on a binary gate (now abstains, pinned); and `parseFloat(x) || null` turned a real `0` replaced-API into `null`, which reads as "not recorded" (now credits the full API, pinned).

**One stop-condition call, ruled after the fact so it is not re-litigated.** Brief section 7 said stop if a rules arm validates policy body fields at create. `firestore.rules:367` DOES - `sourceOfProspect`, `dateWritten`, `proposedAPI` - but it has since life-pipeline slice 1A, which predates this brief. **The tripwire was already tripped before the work started, which makes it a badly written tripwire, not a finding.** What it guarded does not apply: `replacedPolicyAPI` appears in the rules exactly once, in the update arm's `hasOnly` allow-list at line 397, with no value guard. Section 4's wording was the correct one and CC picked it. **The consequence is banked, not waved away: R4's "required" is client-side only, so the lens's `api: 0` + "Replaced API not recorded" branch is a permanent backstop and must never be deleted on the grounds that the form requires the field now.**

**Verification note worth carrying:** bot review was thin on BOTH PRs - CodeRabbit gave #960 a summary with no line-level review (Free plan) and was rate-limited then clean on #961 - so the operator's own read was the review on two money-display surfaces. Two read-only scripts live in `verification/` (gitignored by design) and are re-runnable after every OIPA export: `campaign-window-count.mjs` and `campaign-gap-and-lapses.mjs`.

**What is next, and what gates it.** **C2** (Table 1 accommodation + the retreat readout, Sonnet 5 medium) and **C4** (manager Table 2, Rule 9 clawback flag, Rule 10 award-cash suppression, the page-8 general rules on screen, Opus 5 high) are authorised and NOT started - they were held back because both overlap the merged slices on file, so neither could branch off a clean main until C1 and C3 landed. **Before dispatching C2, amend its brief section:** C3 takes both targets from the TOP tier (Pioneer 825,000/35), which makes the agent card read "73,946 of 825,000" when the operator is really 27% of the way to Champion - the level that decides whether he travels. C2 owns the fix because C2 adds distance-to-next-room. **C5 stays operator-only and nothing renders for a real advisor until it is done:** no campaign document exists in Firestore yet.

MERGED; NO DEPLOY-GATED SURFACE - `git diff --stat e732cd54..4b67bee9 -- firestore.rules firestore.indexes.json functions/` returns EMPTY.** **C2** puts Table 1's accommodation on a tier (`'shared'|'single'|'double'|null`, passed through `sanitizeTiers`, never multiplied by the gate because half a room does not exist) and builds the retreat readout: the cash on offer, the room on offer, and the distance to the next room, with the gate stated in the same breath. **The slice shipped item 7's own failure case first, and the operator caught it in review.** `CampaignCard` totalled WEEKLY SUBMISSIONS while `CampaignLensPanel` read the ledger, so for an operator whose entire book is imported the dashboard said **0 API / 0 apps / "275,000 more API and 35 more apps"** while the ledger said **73,946.28 / 3 / "201,054 and 32"** - two screens, two distances to the same prize, which is exactly what item 7 was written to forbid. Worse, the screenshot deliverable hid it: the harness hand-wrote `const LIVE = [{ agentId: 'op', apiSold: 73946.28, applicationsSold: 3 }]` and fed it as `submissions`, so the card was photographed being handed numbers the real card never receives. **Fixed in `f7134cb7` before merge:** `CampaignCard` now calls `derivePolicyLens` - the SAME call the lens panel makes, not merely the same helper. **One trap inside the fix, and the dispatcher's own prescription walked into it:** the obvious move is to reuse HomeV2's existing `policies` array, but that is `excludeImported(...)` and the operator's book is 100% imported, so it is EMPTY - it would have produced 0/0 by a second route and looked like a fix. `AgentDashboard` now derives two arrays from one fetch, `policiesAll` (raw, for the campaign card) beside `policies` (filtered, for everything else), and a test pins it: "an EMPTY array - what excludeImported would have produced - reads 0/0". **Two pieces of the builder's own verification were also wrong and were replaced:** the surprise-stop probe compared VERDICTS (`null` vs `null`, agreeing only because both were below the entry rung) instead of FIGURES, and a test fed the same numbers through the shared helper twice and asserted they matched - a tautology. **Item 6 also landed:** both lens targets now come from `tierNext`, the tier in reach, not the ladder's ceiling, so the bar reads 27% toward Champion rather than 9% of a Pioneer level the advisor has never been shown. **#963, merged alongside, is materially connected to C1 rather than incidental:** the persistency entry window moves from a fixed three months to year-to-date with a three-month floor, because Tatil's report lags by MONTHS not weeks (the June and July 2026 reports both arrived in September 2026, and a three-month window could not reach June, making published figures unenterable). The floor is what keeps November and December of a closing year reachable in January - **exactly the months that settle the Christmas Campaign's December gate.** **One locked-decision carve-out to note, not a silent drift:** Workflow rules say verification artifacts stay local and `verification/` is gitignored by design; C2 un-ignored `verification/c2/*.png` because the screenshots ARE the slice's named deliverable, with a comment stating a future capture replaces the directory rather than accumulating beside it.

| [#961](https://github.com/Kelsean868/agencytrack/pull/961) | `f613c5a3` | **feat(campaign): C1 - the persistency gate becomes per-campaign config.** `normalizeGate(campaign)` resolves `{ mode: 'bands'\|'binary', threshold, basis: 'periodAggregate'\|'finalMonth' }` with today's behaviour as the default, so **a campaign doc with no `persistencyGate` key derives byte-identically to before**. `persistencyPctForGate(records, campaign)` is the ONLY function consumers call; `persistencyPctAtFinalMonth` reads the single record whose `monthKey` equals the campaign's end month and **abstains (`null`) when it is absent rather than falling back to the period aggregate** - Rule 5 says "at the Final Month", and a six-month aggregate is a different number that can land an advisor on the other side of a cliff. Threaded through all four gate surfaces (`CampaignPanel`, `CampaignStandings`, `MeetingMode` CampaignScene, the kiosk panel) plus the builder form, which gains *Gate style* and *Read persistency* controls whose defaults write NO key. **Why this slice existed at all:** the shipped `PERSISTENCY_GATE_BANDS` were lifted from the PREVIOUS Christmas campaign (90/85/80/DQ), and the signed 2026 document has a cliff - `>= 90%` pays 100%, `< 90%` is Disqualified, no partial bands, cash AND accommodation both. The four-combination evidence table shows the two bases DISAGREEING on the brief's fixture (December `0.89`, aggregate `0.91`): bands/period 100%, bands/final 50%, binary/period 100%, binary/final **DQ**. **One real bug caught by the new tests, not by review:** `Number(null)` is `0`, so a blank persistency month read as 0% and DISQUALIFIED on a binary gate; it now abstains (multiplier 1, band `null`, no DQ), pinned. 217 new test lines in `campaignGate.test.js` + 75 in `PersistencyGateStrip.test.jsx`. **No `firestore.rules`, no `functions/`.** |

## Archived from CONTEXT.md § Where we left off — 2026-09-23 fill (PR #970)

**PRIOR - 2026-09-20 (evening). Campaign C2 merged as `4b67bee9` (#962), with #963 `0b2dda5e` alongside. Three of the four build slices are done; C4 is the last, and one follow-up goes before or with it. MERGED; NO DEPLOY-GATED SURFACE - QUERIED across `e732cd54..4b67bee9`, which covers both PRs.**

C2 gave the campaign its retreat: accommodation on a tier, and a readout that states the cash on offer, the room on offer and the distance to the next room in one breath with the gate, because Rule 5 removes the room as well as the cash and half a room does not exist.

**The slice shipped its own brief's failure case, and the operator caught it in review rather than a test.** Item 7 exists to stop an advisor being told two different things on two screens. `CampaignCard` totalled weekly submissions; `CampaignLensPanel` read the ledger. For an operator whose whole book is imported that is **0 API / 0 apps** on the dashboard against **TTD 73,946.28 / 3 apps** in the ledger - two distances to the same prize. The screenshot deliverable hid the split instead of exposing it: the harness hand-wrote `const LIVE = [{ agentId: 'op', apiSold: 73946.28, applicationsSold: 3 }]` and passed it as `submissions`, so the card was photographed holding numbers the real card never receives. **The lesson is about the evidence, not the code: a deliverable rendered from invented input proves nothing, and naming that input `LIVE` made it read as proof.**

**The fix, and the trap inside it.** The card now calls `derivePolicyLens` - the same call as the lens, not merely the same helper. The obvious way to feed it is HomeV2's existing `policies`, and **that is wrong in a way that looks right**: it is `excludeImported(...)`, and with a 100%-imported book it is EMPTY, so it returns 0/0 by a second route. The dispatcher prescribed exactly that in the fix instruction and the builder caught it. `AgentDashboard` now derives `policiesAll` (raw, campaign card) beside `policies` (filtered, everything else) from one fetch, pinned by a test that names the trap.

**Two pieces of the builder's own verification were also wrong, and both had passed while the product was broken:** the surprise-stop probe compared verdicts (`null` vs `null` - agreeing only because both sat below the entry rung) instead of figures, and a test pushed the same numbers through the shared helper twice and asserted they matched. A tautology cannot fail. Both were replaced with assertions that run against policy shapes.

**#963 is not incidental to this track.** The persistency entry window moves from a fixed three months to year-to-date with a three-month floor, because Tatil's report lags by MONTHS - June and July 2026 both landed in September 2026, and a three-month window could not reach June. The floor is what keeps November and December of a closing year reachable in January, which is exactly how a December gate gets its figure entered. C1's `finalMonth` basis depends on that record existing.

**What is left.** **C4** - manager Table 2, the Rule 9 clawback flag, Rule 10 award-cash suppression, and the page-8 general rules on screen (Opus 5, high effort) - is authorised and not started. **One follow-up goes before or with it:** the #948 source-tree guard now passes `AgentDashboard.jsx` on a text match while that file hands an unfiltered array downstream, so wiring `policiesAll` into awards or production would earn an imported book retroactive credit with nothing going red. C4 is the next slice near `awardsEngine.js`. Full body in `docs/FOLLOW_UPS.md`, banked `e720015f`.

**And the thing no build can do: C5.** There is still **no campaign document in Firestore**, so C1-C3 render nothing for a real advisor. Before authoring it, delete the **8 `shakedown` fixtures** sitting in `tenants/tatillife_south/campaigns`. When the real campaign does exist, expect the honest number: **3 apps against 35**, because that is what the ledger holds as at the 15 September export. Applications bind, not API.

## Archived from CONTEXT.md § Where we left off — 2026-09-24 fill (PR #971)

**PRIOR - 2026-09-23. Hero-ledger H1 merged as `594be38a` (#968). Every production hero now reads the Policy Ledger, not weekly reports. MERGED; NO DEPLOY-GATED SURFACE - QUERIED across `43ec8c52..594be38a`.**

The goal in one line: Kyron's hero numbers should say what his ledger says, not what a self-reported weekly figure says - three sources (weekly `ytdTotals`, the Policy Ledger's lifetime hero, and Awards' `confirmedSettlements`/`excludeImported` split) disagreed with each other and none of them was TTD 87,146.28, the actual settled figure a read-only probe found in his 229-policy live book.

**`ledgerProduction.js` is the one place R2/R3/R4/R5 live.** `deriveYearProduction(policies, { year, weekStarting, submissions })` returns settled and submitted API/apps for the year, decided by `dateIssued` for settled and by `dateSubmitted` -> `dateWritten` -> `dateIssued` (flagged `datedByIssue`) for submitted - never by `importSource`. R3's production-credit rule is NOT a second table: it is the campaign's Rule 7 table with the one row where the two rulings disagree replaced (lump sums earn 10% API generally, 0% under the signed campaign document), expressed by letting `creditFor()`'s row shape carry a fraction instead of only `'full'`/`'none'` - no campaign row uses a fraction, so C3's 40 existing credit tests pass byte-for-byte unchanged.

**The heroes, not just the number, moved.** HomeV2's `HeroCard` keeps Settled API as the big number (the goal bar and MDRT marker still target it) and adds Settled apps, Submitted API and Submitted apps beneath it, with a "dated by issue" marker so nobody reads an OIPA export date as a real submission date. Under that, the R4 reconciliation note states both figures side by side and flags anything over a TTD 1 gap, linking straight to the ledger's create form - and says "Matches your weekly reports" when they agree, never hiding the note either way. `PipelineStrip`'s old "TOTAL - YTD" was a lifetime sum, lapsed and NTU included, under a YTD label; it is now the year's real Settled/Submitted API, and the six stage tiles that DO stay lifetime counts say so - WHOLE BOOK.

**Two gaps surfaced after the PR was open, both fixed in the same PR under a dispatcher-authorised Rule 9 extension rather than a follow-up.** `GoalsPanel.jsx` carried its OWN third copy of the weekly-report YTD sum, unnamed by the brief, disagreeing with the dashboard hero for the same producing manager - it now takes ManagerDashboard's `useMyProduction()` result as a prop and reads no submissions or settlements of its own. And the home hero was static after a ledger write: add a policy, return to Home, see the old number until a reload. `PolicyLedgerPanel` now fires `onPoliciesChanged` after create/transition/import; `AgentDashboard` marks its policy copy stale and refetches on the next Home visit. A new test adds a policy (hero moves), then lapses it (hero returns to the original figure), proving the refresh rather than asserting it.

**The smoke that closed the PR ran on Kyron's own live account, not a fixture.** Read-only, both themes, 30/30 checks: Settled 5 policies/TTD 87,146.28, Submitted 6/TTD 123,146.28 "dated by issue", the reconciliation note and its mismatch flag, the ledger header and Goals both matching the hero, the campaign card unchanged at TTD 73,946.28/3 apps. Two rounds of the script itself were wrong first, not the app - the hero numeral counts up on load and the first run read it mid-animation (TTD 72,605.52), and the campaign card loads async and was checked before it rendered - both fixed by waiting for the settled value rather than reading once. The first ledger screenshot also showed client names from the policy list beneath the header card; deleted before commit, and the smoke now clips the screenshot to the header only.

**CodeRabbit gave no review on this PR.** Free-plan rate limit, and per the FOLLOW_UPS finding banked on #966 a rate-limited pass on that plan cannot be recovered by any known remedy - recorded as "no bot review ran", not as a clean pass.

**What is left.** **H2** (awards count current-year imported business by date, amending ruling 5e for the ledger path in `AgentAwardsPanel.jsx`) is authorised, Fable 5.1 pinned, and dispatches next. **H3** (the campaign hero card on Awards and Dashboard, fed by `derivePolicyLens`) is Sonnet 5 and starts after H2 merges. **One gap both inherit:** the hero-refresh extension is wired for the agent dashboard only - a producing manager who writes a policy and returns to Goals still needs a reload; not blocking, not yet fixed. **Banked, out of scope for all three PRs:** the Firestore `INTERNAL ASSERTION FAILED (ID: b815)` cache crash from `persistentMultipleTabManager`, per the brief's own instruction.

## Archived from CONTEXT.md § Where we left off — 2026-09-26 fill (PR #977)

**PRIOR - 2026-09-24. Persistency outlook merged as `d7d449ac` (#971) - derived, confirmed and live-estimated persistency now reach the Persistency tab hero, CampaignHeroCard's preview and the Home pulse chip from one shared module. MERGED; NO DEPLOY-GATED SURFACE - QUERIED across `904a38e0..d7d449ac`.**

The goal in one line: Kyron had no 24-month persistency record saved, so the campaign card's gate row read "-" and could not warn him, even though the ledger can already derive the figure inside the Persistency tab's own entry form - which will not save until four "not in export" inputs are typed. Kyron's own ruling (23 Sep 2026): show the head-office-derived month as **derived**, let him **confirm** it against the HO report later, and show a separate **estimated today** figure that moves as he keys policies.

**`src/lib/persistency/persistencyOutlook.js` calls the shipped `deriveFromLedger`/`deriveAll`/`calculateShortfall` - it never re-implements the formula.** It returns `confirmed` (a saved record), `derived` (the last full month before the export date, HO export ONLY), `estimateToday` (the current month, export plus hand-keyed policies), `ifPendingSettle` (estimateToday with every pending policy treated as placed - shown only when one exists, because a submitted policy is not placed), `gateMonth` (the campaign's own gate month projected on today's ledger, with the gap to the threshold), and `assumptions` naming which of the four manual inputs the export cannot supply were taken from a saved record versus assumed 0.

**A mid-PR dispatcher decision (ruling B, 23 Sep 2026) restricts when Confirm may open.** The derived figure is always reckoned on the 24-month window, but head office reports every month before September 2026 on the RETIRED 12-month model - so an August derived figure and an August HO report are not the same kind of number and cannot be checked against each other. `derived.confirmable = isTwentyFourMonthModel(monthKey)`; the tab shows no Confirm control on August and states why in the caption, and `ConfirmPersistencySheet` refuses to render at all for a non-confirmable month. Confirm becomes available from September onward, once the next OIPA export lands.

**Phase 0 recon caught a second scope cut before it was built, not after.** The brief's second confirm choice - "HO report says __%", saving a bare head-office percentage - cannot be shipped: `firestore.rules`' `validE3InputsNonNegative()` requires all six money inputs (`businessPlaced`, `notTakens`, `incPPPs`, `lumpsums100`, `lapses`, `reinstatements`) on every create/update, so a doc holding only a percentage is denied by rules before it reaches Firestore. Choice 1 ("Matches the HO report") ships alone, exactly as the brief allowed; a differing HO figure is routed to Self-entry instead.

**A real gap surfaced in Phase 1, not assumed away.** Hand-keyed policies (typed by the agent in the Policy Ledger form, never imported) were invisible to `deriveFromLedger`: they store `dateIssued` as a Firestore Timestamp rather than a `YYYY-MM-DD` string and never set `isWritingAgent`, so every one was silently dropped as "not the writing agent" or "no issue date" - the exact defect the brief's `estimateToday` line exists to fix. `toLedgerDoc()` normalises both fields before deriving; `deriveFromLedger` itself is untouched, per the brief's own "out of scope" line. Kyron has 0 hand-keyed policies in production today, so this path is unit-tested (a hand-keyed SETTLED policy raises `estimateToday`; a SUBMITTED one does not, but raises `ifPendingSettle`) rather than live-exercised.

**A read-only live check against Kyron's book reproduces the brief's own table to the cent** (export 15 Sep 2026, annuity rule `ignore`, all four manual inputs 0): derived August 89.6% (296,457.24 placed / 30,878.88 lapses), estimated-today September 86.6% (210,975.24 / 28,196.88), December projection 85.7% (188,595.72 / 27,014.52). The gap sentence originally named the campaign's TOP tier (Pioneer, TTD 825,000) rather than the smallest tier that actually closes it - caught in the first smoke run and fixed in `8d213922` before the second: it now correctly names Champion (TTD 275,000 settled, since 73,946.28 is already credited) as the smallest tier whose remaining API covers the TTD 81,549.48 gap.

**Two read-only smoke walks on Kyron's live account, light + dark, one per fix commit: 24 PASS / 0 FAIL both times.** Screenshots in `verification/persistency-outlook/`, card-cropped, no client names. Confirmed on screen across both runs: Derived Aug 89.6% (no Confirm on the first-commit run before ruling B, then Confirm absent with the stated reason on the ruling-B run); Estimated today 86.6% with the assumptions popover listing the 4 assumed-0 inputs, rule `ignore`, export 15 Sep; December 85.7% with the gap sentence naming Champion; the campaign row reading "89.6% derived, Aug 2026 / 90%" in warning tokens; the Home pulse chip matching. Confirm was checked for presence/absence only - never clicked, so no real persistency record was written by the smoke; the save path is proven by mocked-service component tests instead.

## Archived from CONTEXT.md § Where we left off — 2026-09-26 fill (PR #982)

**PRIOR - 2026-09-26 (later still). P2a server integrity merged AND DEPLOYED as `5faf4a6a` (#978) - the overnight run 2026-09-26 is fully closed, all 3 items shipped.**

The goal in one line: four fixes in `functions/` from the 2026-09-24 audit § 4 — SEC-06 (a branch manager could pass any `branchId` to the Agent-of-the-Month callables and see another branch's candidates), BUG-08 (the leaderboard's read-modify-write could lose updates under concurrent submissions, and a resubmit double-counted points), BUG-09 (the Sunday cron could overwrite a report that had just been submitted, a TOCTOU race), SEC-16 (16 known `functions/` dependency vulnerabilities, including 1 critical and 5 high).

**Built and gated by the overnight orchestrator alongside R1/R2, HELD by design.** Per the governing brief, functions changes are never merged by the orchestrator — P2a stopped at an open PR, labeled "HELD — needs Kyron merge + firebase deploy". Kyron reviewed, merged, and deployed.

**Fixes.** SEC-06: both callables now read the caller's own `branchId` from their user doc for a `branch_manager`, throwing `permission-denied` on any mismatch; SM/TA/PA keep free choice. BUG-08: the leaderboard read-modify-write now runs inside `runTransaction()`; a new map field `awardedBySubmission.{submissionId}` on the leaderboard doc stores the points last awarded per submission, so a resubmit applies only the delta. BUG-09: the submitted-status check and the draft write now happen inside one transaction. SEC-16: `npm audit fix` (no `--force`) took `functions/`'s 16 vulnerabilities (1 critical, 5 high, 9 moderate, 1 low) down to 9 moderate; the remaining chain's only fix is the forbidden `exceljs@3.4.0` downgrade; a new CI step audits root and `functions/` at `--audit-level=high`.

**Gates all green before merge:** root lint 0/0; root tests 6,922 passing; functions tests 734 passing; build clean; CI (`lint-and-build`, `functions-tests`, `firestore-rules-tests`, `a11y-contrast`) all pass; CodeRabbit rate-limited on every push, no line-level review ran.

**DEPLOYED — QUERIED, not inferred.** Merge was `2026-09-26T14:06:00Z`. `gcloud functions describe` on all 4 touched functions (`setAgentOfMonth`, `getAgentOfMonthCandidates`, `onSubmissionWrite`, `aggregateDailyToWeekly`) shows `status: ACTIVE` with `updateTime` ~`14:12Z`, six minutes after merge — `setAgentOfMonth` at `versionId: 37`. No function was added, removed, or renamed.

**What is left.** The PR body's post-deploy smoke walk (Agent of the Month candidates load for Kyron's own branch; Firebase Console Functions logs clean for the first hour; Monday's leaderboard shows correct points with no doubling) has NOT been independently run by this fill — it needs Kyron to confirm, or the next session to check the logs. **Banked FU (MEDIUM), unrelated to P2a but still open:** give the A11Y test agent a test campaign so future preview/production smokes can exercise the Campaign screen and R1's campaign card live.
